Conclusion: 因果感知框架的首次实现证明了其在实际公平性评估中的价值，揭示了多专家决策中不同因果视角会导致不同的公平性判断。这强调了在公平性分析中必须考虑智能体间结构性分歧的重要性。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [237] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer在推理阶段的新解读：它不是"随机鹦鹉"式的统计复述，而是通过推理期间动态生成的参数，构建并应用依赖于提示的变换（SIDPP——序列级交互式动态并行处理）。这种动态处理随提示长度增长而增强，甚至可能超过静态处理（强提示敏感性），并推测人脑语言处理可能也以类似机制实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"随机鹦鹉"观点，即认为大型语言模型仅仅是复现训练中习得的统计规律；作者主张Transformer在推理时能够动态生成参数并构建提示相关的变换，这为理解其工作机制提供了新的理论框架。

Method: 提出SIDPP框架：将token向量视为"被变换的概念"，由矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换视为"变换的概念"；这些变换可能是静态的（训练固定）或动态的（从输入序列生成）。在机制上，这些变换对应一组简单神经网络，其创新在于"输出-权重互连"——部分网络的输出决定其他网络的权重。这一机制使系统能从提示中构建变换并修改token表示。

Result: 动态处理对整体计算的贡献随提示长度增长，可能等于或超过静态处理，这一现象称为"强提示敏感性"。该框架对可解释性、可预测性、可控性以及设计更小更可持续的系统均有启示意义。

Conclusion: 由于人脑神经系统具备实现SIDPP所需的机制，论文推测人类语言处理可能本身就是一种SIDPP，由与Transformer功能架构相关似的皮层结构所实现。

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [238] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT框架，通过构建两个互补的分类体系（教师策略和学生学习行为），标注260段真实师生对话，并利用监督微调与分类对齐的GRPO算法后训练Qwen3.5-4B模型，产出能够进行教学适应性互动的ESL辅导机器人TACTutor，在基准测试和人工评测中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛用于ESL学习者的对话练习，但有效的ESL辅导不仅需要流利的回复生成，还需要根据学习者行为和对话情境选择合适的教学动作。现有的人本辅导研究虽然提供了适应性支持的原则，但这些原则通常是任务特定的，并未充分整合到基于LLM的ESL辅导训练和评估中。

Method: 1) 基于已有文献构建两个互补分类体系：含13种辅导策略的Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy和按动作类型与状态刻画学习者行为的Student-Move Taxonomy；2) 构建TACTCorpus，对260段真实师生对话进行32,379个标注并加入质量控制的增广训练数据；3) 使用监督微调和分类对齐的Group Relative Policy Optimization对Qwen3.5-4B进行后训练，优化脚手架质量而非仅参考模仿。

Result: 在包含78个真实辅导情境的策略平衡诊断基准TACTBench上，TACTutor相比其基础模型提升20.30%，且在相同协议下优于所有评测的专有基线模型；在外部教育基准上保持基础模型性能；50名学习者的盲评研究中获得最高总体平均评分。

Conclusion: TACT框架为开发教学适应性的ESL辅导机器人提供了开放基础，包括数据、基准和模型权重均公开发布，其分类体系驱动的训练方法可有效提升LLM的教学适应性而非仅模仿参考回复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [239] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现由基础模型驱动的自主代理在社交困境中会稳定收敛到合作，这与经典博弈论预测的相互背叛相矛盾，并提出"嵌入式贝叶斯代理"理论模型和"嵌入式均衡"概念来解释和替代这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型代理被广泛集成到社会和经济系统中，理解其集体行为规律对安全和合作至关重要。经典博弈论基于"解耦主体"假设，而现代AI代理会联合预测自身未来行动与外部观察，因此需要新的理论框架来解释其行为。

Method: 首先通过实验展示基础模型代理在风格化社会困境中通过最优规划一致收敛到稳定合作；随后提出"嵌入式贝叶斯代理"理论模型，将代理建模为把自己视为宇宙一部分、对自身决策算法保持认知不确定性的实体；最后通过"嵌入式均衡"概念形式化相似性推断机制。

Result: 基础模型代理在社交困境中表现出稳定合作行为，直接违背经典博弈论的相互背叛预测；嵌入式代理通过推断他人行为相似性，将自己的决策作为证据（决定合作预测类似伙伴也合作），从而达成合作。

Conclusion: 嵌入式均衡取代纳什均衡，为现代AI代理的社交行为提供新的博弈论基础，标志着从"解耦主体"到"嵌入式主体"的理论范式转变。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [240] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应测试时扩展方法，利用轻量级模糊控制器将提示复杂度与模型置信度等可解释信号映射到每个查询的采样预算，在保持与固定全预算相当性能的同时减少平均采样数，使推理计算开销可解释且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM测试时扩展多采用固定每查询预算，对简单和困难提示投入相同计算量，效率低下且难以解释为何特定提示需要特定数量的样本。

Method: 设计轻量级模糊控制器，将估计的提示复杂度和模型置信度等可解释信号映射到每查询采样预算；简单或高置信提示分配较少样本，困难或低置信提示分配较多样本。在匹配解码设置和受控答案选择下进行公平对齐评估。

Result: 在问答和数学推理任务上，自适应模糊控制优于多个标准基线（如best-of-N、计算感知扩展、自置信度基线），与选择器匹配的全预算控制接近，同时减少了平均采样数量。

Conclusion: 可解释的自适应采样是提升大语言模型测试时推理效率的实用方向，既保持性能又降低计算成本。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [241] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HIVE（人类输入变体引擎），一套涵盖语音转写扰动和QWERTY键盘扰动的测试工具，用于评估大语言模型对人类输入噪声的鲁棒性。研究发现语音扰动显著降低指令微调模型的准确率，且代价来自转写的结构而非填充词；键盘扰动代价较小，模型能吸收大量噪声。两类扰动的核心伤害机制是原始token的破坏而非新增token。语音干扰的损害出现在需要构造或推理答案的任务中（多选题无影响）；伤害不只是来自测试集污染；轻量适应无法消除；思考预算几乎完全恢复键盘通道性能但对语音通道无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过打字或语音两种方式向语言模型输入信息，每种通道都带有独特的噪声特征（键盘的拼写错误、语音的转写不流利和使用AI辅助听写工具产生的结构调整），但这些扰动如何影响LLM的性能仍未被系统研究。

Method: 构建HIVE测试套件，包含语音转写扰动（常规转写的口语不流利与AI听写工具的文本重构）和QWERTY键盘拼写扰动，在多个指令微调模型上进行系统评估，并设计控制实验隔离填充词、token破坏、任务类型等变量。

Result: 七项发现：语音扰动在所有测试模型上都降低准确率且代价来自转写结构而非填充词；键盘扰动代价更小且模型能吸收大量噪声；两者共同机制是原始token被破坏而非新token加入；通道差距仅出现在构造或推理类任务中，多选题无差异；伤害不单来自测试集污染；轻量适应无法消除；思考预算几乎完全恢复键盘通道但语音通道仍受影响，压缩语音反而更差。

Conclusion: 语音和键盘输入通道的扰动对LLM性能有不同影响，其核心机制是原始token的破坏。语音输入的鲁棒性问题比键盘更深层，无法通过轻量适应解决，现有推理增强手段对语音通道无效，需针对不同输入通道设计专门的鲁棒性策略。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [242] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReflectRL框架，利用专家模型失败轨迹（称为"黄金负轨迹"）作为反思信号，而非直接丢弃，通过反思推理和策略迁移提升大语言模型的推理能力，在9个基准、4种模型骨架和4种on-policy训练方法上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹引导的on-policy训练方法依赖专家模型的成功轨迹作为监督信号，但当专家在更难问题上失败时，这些失败轨迹通常被当作负样本丢弃，导致监督信号缺失。作者认为这些失败轨迹仍蕴含着有价值的推理信号，可以用于反思学习。

Method: 提出ReflectRL框架，包含两个关键阶段：(1) 利用失败轨迹引反思推理（Reflective Reasoning）；(2) 通过反思到直接策略迁移（Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition）将学到的推理行为迁移回直接推理模式。该框架是轻量级即插即用的。

Result: 在9个基准、4种LLM骨架和4种on-policy训练方法上的实验表明，ReflectRL能够一致性地提升推理性能，且额外开销极小。

Conclusion: 失败轨迹不应被简单丢弃，通过反思视角重新利用它们可以有效提升模型推理能力，且框架具有较好的通用性和兼容性。

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Caved or Convinced: Temporal Sampling Gates Claim Deference in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03160)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Jingling Xue,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文研究了视频大语言模型在判断事件先后顺序时的两类失败模式：误信虚假主张和拒绝真实主张。作者将失败原因区分为"可用性"（采样帧是否包含两个事件）和"加权"（模型是否信任证据超过用户），并提出一种逆转测试（reversal test）来消除模型的顺序先验，将顺序准确率提升至0.92-1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频谄媚（sycophancy）研究只测量模型误信虚假主张的问题，并通过让模型降低对用户的信任来缓解，但这种方法已经被文本和图像模型研究证明会加剧拒绝真实主张的问题。以往文献将两种失败归因于同一原因，作者希望区分"可用性"（采样帧是否包含事件）与"加权"（证据是否被信任）两个独立因素。

Method: 作者设计了两种保持主张不变的干预方法：1）帧保留重排（frame-preserving reorder），翻转主张的真假；2）采样偏移（sampling-offset shift），在固定帧预算下捕获或错过两个事件。在此基础上提出了逆转测试（reversal test），通过对采样帧正序和逆序分别打分，抵消模型的顺序先验，然后根据结果进行回答、重新采样或弃权。

Result: 当事件未被采样到时，九个被评估模型接受真实和虚假主张的比率相同（Youden's J=0）。可用性是必要条件但不充分——九个模型中五个能读取事件顺序，但其中四个仍向虚假主张让步，达到加权上限。逆转测试在能读取顺序的模型上将顺序准确率提升至0.92-1.00，对无法读取顺序的模型则选择弃权而非猜测。

Conclusion: 视频模型在时间顺序判断中的失败不能仅通过调整对用户的信任来解决，因为当证据从未被采样时，信任无法被校准。逆转测试提供了一种不依赖读取用户主张的校准方法，通过区分可用性和加权两个维度来系统性地理解和改进视频模型的时序推理能力。

Abstract: When asked which of two events came first, video large language models can fail in two opposite ways: cave to a false claim, or reject a true one. Prior video sycophancy work measures only the first and mitigates it by teaching the model to trust the user less, a fix known in text and image models to worsen the second. In video, both failures come from two causes the literature treats as one: availability, whether the sparse sampled frames contain the two events, and weighting, whether that evidence is trusted over the user. We separate them with two interventions that keep the claim fixed: a frame-preserving reorder that flips the claim's truth, and a sampling-offset shift that captures or misses both events at a fixed frame budget. When the events are missed, the two twins present identical frames, so each of the nine models we evaluate accepts a true and a false claim at the same rate, making Youden's $J=0$ by construction. Availability is necessary but not sufficient. Five of the nine read the order, yet four of those five still cave to the false claim, so their deference hits a weighting ceiling. Since trust cannot be calibrated over evidence that was never sampled, we propose a reversal test that cancels the model's order prior by scoring the sampled frames forward and reversed, then answers, resamples, or abstains without reading the claim. The test raises the order accuracy to 0.92-1.00 on the models that read the order and abstains rather than guesses on those that cannot.

</details>


### [244] [Hear to See: Discerning Stateful Listening for Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03264)
*Leiye Liu,Miao Zhang,Jiahong Jiang,Jingjing Li,Jialong Zhong,Kai Peng,Tingwei Liu,Wei Ji,Yongri Piao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Hear to See (H2S)方法，用于解决音视频实例分割中的重叠声源对应和异步动态跟踪两个核心难题，在AVISeg上达到SOTA性能（48.54 mAP，超越先前方法7.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频实例分割方法难以匹配重叠声学事件与视觉实例，且无法处理音视频信号在时间上不对齐的问题。论文旨在解决声源与视觉实例的精确对应以及异步音视频动态下的鲁棒跟踪两大关键问题。

Method: 提出H2S框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 声学语义投影器（ASP）用于解耦混合音频，并建立从语义域到空间域的层级对应关系；(2) 异步动态调制器（ADM）通过音频调制的Mamba架构自适应调整状态转移，在动态变化时优先当前信息，在稳定时期保持连续性。

Result: 在AVISeg数据集上，H2S使用COCO预训练的ResNet50达到了48.54 mAP，超越先前最佳方法7.8%，实现了SOTA性能。代码将在论文被接收后开源。

Conclusion: H2S通过解耦混合音频建立语义到空间的层级对应，并利用音频调制的Mamba机制处理异步音视频动态，有效解决了音视频实例分割中的重叠声源匹配与时间异步跟踪问题，取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Audio-visual instance segmentation (AVIS) requires accurately identifying and tracking individual sounding objects with pixel-level masks. Existing methods struggle to match overlapping acoustic events with visual instances and handle asynchronous audio-visual dynamics. Therefore, two critical questions arise: how can a model establish precise correspondence between overlapping sound sources and visual instances, and how can a model maintain robust tracking when audio and visual signals are temporally misaligned?This paper proposes Hear to See (H2S), addressing these challenges through two mechanisms. The Acoustic-Semantic Projector (ASP) disentangles mixed audio and establishes hierarchical correspondence from semantic to spatial domains. The Asynchronous Dynamics Modulator (ADM) adaptively adjusts state transitions via audio-modulated Mamba, prioritizing current information during dynamic variations and maintaining continuity in stable periods.Experiments on AVISeg show H2S achieves SOTA performance, attaining 48.54 mAP with a COCO pretrained ResNet50 and surpassing the previous by 7.8\%. The code will be open-sourced once the paper is accepted. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/leiyeliu/H2S.

</details>


### [245] [Balancing Efficiency and Efficacy: Training-Free Attention-Guided Switching Between Explicit and Latent Thoughts for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03450)
*Haoqian Kang,Liupeng Li,Kuofeng Gao,Jinpeng Wang,Zhenyu Lu,Bin Chen,Ke Chen,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的推理策略AGS（Attention-Guided Switching），通过视觉到文本注意力比率动态评估MLLM的认知焦点，在感知token上触发潜在推理以保留视觉信息，在逻辑token上强制显式文本生成以保证结构锚定，从而解决多模态大语言模型推理中感知不确定性与逻辑不确定性混淆的问题，在提升准确率的同时减少自回归步数和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的推理方法存在痛点：显式的文本链式思维（CoT）计算开销大且容易产生视觉幻觉，而现有的潜在推理方法通常需要昂贵的训练成本。直接将在纯语言模型中无需训练的推理机制迁移到多模态场景会造成性能不稳定，其根本原因在于这些机制依赖token级别的熵，该指标混淆了感知模糊性（如视觉细节不清）与逻辑不确定性（如复杂推理步骤）。因此需要一种能解耦感知与推理的无需训练推理策略。

Method: 本文提出一种新的指标——视觉到文本注意力比率（vision-to-text attention ratio），用于动态衡量模型的认知焦点。基于该指标，提出的框架AGS（Attention-Guided Switching）自适应地为感知token触发潜在推理（在连续空间中保留高保真视觉信息），同时为逻辑token强制启用显式文本生成（保持结构锚定）。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能，在提升准确率的同时显著提高了推理效率，减少了自回归步数和延迟。

Conclusion: 通过解耦感知与推理两个认知层面，AGS为MLLM提供了一种高效且无需训练的推理新范式，能够兼顾视觉信息保真与逻辑结构稳定，为多模态推理领域提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires both fine-grained visual perception and rigorous logical deduction. Explicit text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is computationally expensive and prone to visual hallucinations, while existing latent reasoning methods typically require costly training. Furthermore, directly adapting training-free LLM reasoning mechanisms to the multimodal setting yields unstable performance. We identify that this failure stems from their reliance on token-level entropy, which fundamentally conflates perceptual ambiguity (e.g., unclear visual details) with logical uncertainty (e.g., complex reasoning steps). To overcome this bottleneck, we present a novel training-free inference strategy for MLLMs that explicitly decouples perception and reasoning. We propose a novel metric, the vision-to-text attention ratio, to dynamically gauge the model's cognitive focus. Guided by this metric, our proposed framework, Attention-Guided Switching (AGS), adaptively triggers latent reasoning for perceptual tokens to preserve high-fidelity visual information in the continuous space, while enforcing explicit text generation for logical tokens to maintain structural anchoring. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly improving both accuracy and inference efficiency by reducing autoregressive steps and latency. Code is released at https://github.com/swordAndSnow/MM26-AGS.

</details>


### [246] [Adaptive Modality Reliability Diagnosis and Restoration for Robust Multimodal Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03475)
*Suraj Kumar,Mohnish Raj,Soumi Chattopadhayay,Chandranath Adak,Ayan Dutta*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: PRIME是一个闭环可靠性引导框架，用于多模态意图识别，通过诊断、修复和重新评估各模态质量，在缺失、噪声、冲突和模态不平衡条件下提升鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据下的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态意图识别方法隐式推断模态重要性，仅通过重加权或抑制不可靠输入来处理噪声、缺失或冲突的模态，但没有判断退化的模态是否可以被修复并重新信任。

Method: PRIME通过上下文对数方差表示每个模态的弱点，该方差从预测置信度、认知分歧、跨模态共识和特征退化等诊断证据中估计。由于缺乏模态可靠性标注，估计器使用受控模态损坏（已知退化严重程度）和异方差不确定性目标进行显式训练。对不可靠模态，PRIME使用估计的弱点控制原型条件变分修复模块，从互补模态重建退化表示。修复后重新估计可靠性，决定修复后的表示是否足以贡献于预测，并使用修复后精度进行逆方差多模态融合。

Result: 在多模态意图识别基准上的实验表明，PRIME在保持竞争性干净数据性能的同时，在缺失、噪声、冲突和模态不平衡条件下显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过"诊断-修复-重新评估"的闭环机制，PRIME不仅能识别不可靠模态，还能判断其是否可被修复并重新信任，解决了现有方法只处理而不修复的问题，为多模态鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal intent recognition combines linguistic, acoustic, and visual evidence, but individual modalities may be noisy, missing, semantically conflicting, or disproportionately dominant. Existing methods typically infer modality importance implicitly and either reweight or suppress unreliable inputs, without determining whether a degraded modality can be repaired and subsequently trusted. We propose PRIME (Precision-weighted Reliability Inference and Modality rEstoration), a closed-loop reliability guided framework that jointly diagnoses, restores, and reassesses modality quality at the sample level. PRIME represents the weakness of each modality through a contextual log-variance estimated from complementary diagnostic evidence, including predictive confidence, epistemic disagreement, cross-modal consensus, and feature degeneracy. Because modality-reliability annotations are unavailable, the estimator is explicitly trained using controlled modality corruption with known degradation severity, together with a heteroscedastic uncertainty objective. Rather than directly discarding an unreliable modality, PRIME uses its estimated weakness to control a prototype-conditioned variational restoration module that reconstructs the degraded representation from complementary modalities. Crucially, reliability is re-estimated after restoration, allowing the model to determine whether the repaired representation has become sufficiently trustworthy to contribute to prediction. The resulting post-restoration precisions are used for inverse-variance multimodal fusion. Experiments on multimodal intent-recognition benchmarks show that PRIME maintains competitive clean-data performance while improving robustness under missing, noisy, conflicting, and modality-imbalanced conditions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DDRSR（Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression）方法，通过严格的数学推导和证明，扩展了符号回归中表达式分解与化简的适用范围，避免了暴力搜索子表达式，在分解和数值回归任务上均取得了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归的机器学习方法缺乏对数学和物理原理的深入理解。AI Feynman方法虽然利用了数据的数学性质，但其表达式化简机制适用范围窄，且依赖暴力搜索子表达式，严重限制了实用性。因此需要一种更通用、更高效且具有严格理论保证的表达式分解与化简方法。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导和证明，提出DDRSR方法。该方法从理论上拓宽了表达式分解与化简的适用性，避免了暴力搜索子结构的需求，同时保证了广泛的通用性和严格的理论正确性。

Result: 实验评估表明，DDRSR在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得了显著的性能优势，验证了理论原则的有效性。

Conclusion: DDRSR在符号回归领域展示出更广泛的适用性和理论可靠性，作者还讨论了该范式的适用场景、固有限制以及未来研究方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [248] [Multimodal Auto-regressive Transformer Surrogate for Modeling Variable Operations and Quantifying Uncertainty in Geological Carbon Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02629)
*Yifu Han,Louis J. Durlofsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于模拟地质碳封存操作（可变射孔和注入策略）在断层系统多含水层场景下的动态过程，并通过分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛数据同化实现参数不确定性显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变井射孔和注入策略能够提高地质碳封存操作的效率，但在地质不确定性下对这类复杂多模态操作进行高效建模仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 构建多模态自回归Transformer代理模型：通过三个独立编码器分别处理3D地质模型、相对渗透率标量参数和控制变量三种输入模态，经自注意力融合后由时间解码器通过交叉注意力自回归生成预测。采用4000个GEOS流动模拟进行训练。

Result: 在新测试集上，代理模型实现中位饱和均方误差0.028，其他关注量的中位相对误差为0.2-5%，并能准确捕获从速率控制到井底压力控制的切换。在分层MCMC数据同化中，断层渗透率等关键元参数的不确定性显著降低，后验预测与真实模型结果总体一致。

Conclusion: 该多模态Transformer代理模型为地质碳封存操作在不确定性下的预测和同化提供了高效且准确的计算工具，尤其擅长处理多模态输入和复杂控制策略场景。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [249] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化电路追踪（circuit tracing）的流水线，通过让语言模型直接对特征描述进行分组来生成supernode，无需人工标注。实验表明自动化生成的supernode与人工标注同样可解释，在两个跳点任务中97/100的提示能恢复中间跳点对应的supernode，并展示了开放探索场景下的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪是揭示语言模型内部计算的有力技术，但现有的做法需要耗时的人工步骤来将单个特征或MLP神经元分组为supernode，这限制了电路追踪的规模化和广泛应用。

Method: 提出一个简单流水线：直接将特征描述呈现给语言模型，由模型自动将特征分组为supernode。使用自动化可解释性指标评估supernode质量，并在两跳首都任务和开放探索（1000个Wikipedia属性图自动标注+LLM法官筛选）两个场景中验证。

Result: 自动化生成的supernode与人工标注者生成的supernode具有同等的可解释性；在两跳首都任务中，97/100个提示能恢复中间跳点对应的supernode；开放探索中展示了对1000个属性图自动标注并用LLM法官筛选可能值得人工审查的图表的可行方案。

Conclusion: 即使简单的自动化也能产生有意义的属性图标注，这项工作为自动化电路追踪的进一步研究提供了动力。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [250] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoID-PINN，一种用于SIRD动力学建模的物理信息神经网络，通过行随机源组成矩阵表示空间依赖，并用空间先验（距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息）进行正则化。实验表明，兼容的先验能有效恢复源组成结构（误差0.099），但强错误设定的先验会导致误差大幅上升（0.577），且轨迹拟合良好并不能保证依赖结构恢复正确。在路易斯安那州64县COVID-19数据上，该模型的点预测精度优于自回归负二项基线，但基线在分布拟合方面更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据中地方传播、报告、输入和外源性感染压力难以区分，需要一种能够同时识别空间依赖结构和估计传播动态的模型。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络（PINN）建模SIRD动力学；用行随机源组成矩阵表示区域间的空间依赖（每行非负且和为1）；引入距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构造的空间先验并对矩阵进行正则化。

Result: 四区域仿真中，兼容距离先验下源组成误差为0.099；无正则化为0.159；强错误设定先验下为0.577。在64县COVID-19数据上，MSE从基线的32,957降至11,468，MAE从70.60降至57.73；但基线NLL更低（5.158 vs 5.346）。15县对比中，县邻接使MSE降低6.85%，MAE降低3.1%。

Conclusion: 结构正则化有效但无法唯一恢复边结构；准确的轨迹拟合不能保证空间依赖结构的正确恢复；在使用模型结果前必须进行先验敏感性和观测模型检验。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [251] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种验证器引导（VG）的工作流程，以ODEFormer为符号骨干网络，利用动力学和物理可接受性标准从多轨迹候选方程池中选择方程，实现对高维物理数据的符号方程发现与可靠预测，并在范德波尔振荡器和涡街脱落等经典问题上验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真仿真成本高昂，机器学习替代模型又缺乏可解释性且泛化能力受限。预训练的Transformer虽能通过将合成ODE轨迹映射为方程来提供可解释的替代方案，但将其可靠迁移到高维物理数据仍是一个未解决的挑战。

Method: 开发了验证器引导（VG）工作流程，以ODEFormer作为符号骨干，通过动力学和物理可接受性标准从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选方程，实现模型迁移。对涡街脱落问题，采用坐标降维和符号发现方法，分别在固定和变化的雷诺数下进行实验。

Result: VG在保留初始条件下的范德波尔振荡器上优于原始ODEFormer工作流程。在涡街脱落实验中，VG在固定参数下发现了能恢复基本脱落振荡器及高次谐波的降阶方程，无需尾流特定的候选库或预设Navier-Stokes结构；跨参数模型能泛化到未观测的参数区间。此外发现重建保真度本身并不决定符号可发现性。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了验证器引导的神经到符号（neural-to-symbolic）方法学，为自然科学中可解释且可物理审计的预测提供了新途径，强调了潜在动力学与骨干网络预训练分布之间的兼容性对符号可发现性的重要性。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [252] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了连续时间异质电子健康记录图（CT-HEG）架构，将ICU住院编码为带类型和2D边属性的时间戳图，并实例化为CHIRP-Net（四层异质GATv2Conv网络）。在MIMIC-IV v3.1上（31,142次ICU住院）实现了AUROC 0.8449±0.0071，集成后达0.8618。消融研究表明反向边连接和边特征对性能关键，而合并异质边类型反而以更少参数量获得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型无法建模不规则采样的类型化关系结构，而现有图模型假设固定间隔输入，二者均无法同时处理ICU数据中"不规则时间采样"与"异构实体类型关系"两个挑战，因此需要一种能统一建模两者的架构。

Method: 提出CT-HEG图模型，将每次ICU住院编码为包含三种节点类型（visit、vital、lab_event）的带类型时间戳图，边属性为2D（t_hours/48和value_norm），无需插值。实例化为CHIRP-Net，即四层异质GATv2Conv网络，并在MIMIC-IV上进行五种子实验的评估与消融研究。

Result: CHIRP-Net五种子平均AUROC为0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209），集成AUROC达0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745）。移除反向边导致AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073，时间注意边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093，合并异质边类型（参数量减少7倍）在所有种子上表现更优。校准后ECE为0.0307。

Conclusion: CT-HEG支持用于不规则EHR数据，双向连通性对模型利用输入至关重要，校准效果合理。但作者强调在外部验证、预指定时间评估和人口公平性审计完成前，不能声称模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [253] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出p-SpheretNorm统一框架，将欧几里得残差连接与GeoNorm置于同一视角下，通过角度收缩机制代替指数映射，在nanoGPT上验证有限p值优于GeoNorm等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是训练深度网络的关键机制，GeoNorm通过黎曼几何将残差流限制在超球面上，但其使用的指数映射只是众多retraction映射中的一种。作者希望探索是否存在更优的retraction选择，将现有方法统一到一个框架中并寻找最佳设计。

Method: 作者证明在超球面上所有retraction族可归结为单一的标量设计选择——即更新幅度到旋转角度的转换方式。基于此提出p-SpheretNorm参数族：p=1对应Proj-SpheretNorm（度量投影），p=2对应Cay-SpheretNorm（Cayley变换），两者均为纯代数运算且严格保范。GeoNorm和恒等映射分别对应参数极限端点。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，所有三种方法（Proj/Cay/有限p）均优于现有轻量级深度连接方案；最佳验证损失出现在有限p值处，而非p趋近于GeoNorm极限的位置，表明指数映射并非球面残差流的最优retraction。

Conclusion: 球面残差流的retraction选择是一个连续谱系，旋转角度饱和的有限p值比无界增长的指数映射表现更好，为深度网络连接机制提供了更广阔的设计空间。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [254] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhenMol框架，一种结构保持的表型感知分子表示学习方法。通过将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享和私有成分，在保留化学结构组织的同时实现表型引导的多模态对齐。实验表明其在分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测上均有提升，且能更好地保持分子邻域结构、减少嵌入失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态表示学习方法在优化跨模态对齐时，往往忽略了化学空间的固有组织，导致分子表示失真、结构信息丢失。这限制了表型药物发现在分子结构—细胞响应功能关系建模上的效果。

Method: PhenMol通过将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享成分和私有成分，在专门的分子分支中保留化学结构，实现表型引导的对齐而不破坏分子邻域组织。该设计在整合细胞表型信息的同时，维持分子结构的内在结构。

Result: 在约3.04×10⁴个分子-细胞形态对上的实验表明，PhenMol在270个生物活性任务上提升了分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测性能。ECFP4结构分析进一步证明其更好地保留了分子邻域并减少了嵌入失真。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了多模态分子表示学习中结构感知约束的重要性，并为药物发现中整合细胞表型和化学知识提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [255] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLOBE（Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction），一种轨迹对齐的coreset选择框架，通过利用多个训练检查点的梯度轨迹捕捉样本在优化各阶段的影响，结合多阶矩匹配、Group LASSO与Elastic Net正则化及非负预算约束来实现全局稀疏加权样本选择，在六个基准和五种架构上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端深度神经网络训练受限于大规模数据集的计算和内存成本。现有基于梯度的coreset选择方法依赖单一模型快照的梯度，并采用贪心或追逐步骤，无法捕捉动态优化过程中的样本影响，也难以处理强相关样本。

Method: GLOBE将样本选择建模为全局优化的稀疏加权问题。每个样本用多个训练检查点构建的梯度轨迹表示；引入多阶匹配目标，同时对齐梯度轨迹的一阶均值和投影非中心二阶矩；结合Group LASSO、Elastic Net正则化和非负预算约束实现组级和样本级稀疏性，并稳定相关轨迹的权重；最后通过类别均衡的Top-K选择保证类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准数据集和五种评估架构上，GLOBE在下游测试准确率方面一致优于现有coreset选择方法，尤其在低保留率场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 结合动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配和结构化稀疏性能够有效提升数据高效学习的效果，验证了GLOBE框架在coreset选择任务中的有效性。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [256] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OptR，一种输出感知的旋转方法，用于优化长上下文大语言模型推理中INT2量化的KV缓存。OptR通过最小化经过输出投影W_O之后的注意力输出误差，并采用注意力等价的键重参数化来减少通道偏移，在多个基准上一致提升了现有方法QuaRot和OSCAR的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的INT2量化方法在完整注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，但模型最终受到经过注意力和输出投影W_O传播的误差影响，存在优化目标与真实误差传播之间的不匹配。

Method: OptR将W_O之后的注意力输出误差分解为键诱导项和值诱导项，通过完整的INT2量化和注意力路径学习逐头正交修正；同时应用注意力等价的键重参数化来减少大的通道偏移而不改变softmax分布。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理与编码基准上，OptR一致提升了QuaRot和OSCAR的性能，并增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保持paged KV缓存格式且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: OptR通过输出感知的优化策略有效解决了INT2 KV缓存量化中优化目标与真实误差不匹配的问题，证明了考虑注意力输出端误差传播对低比特量化的重要性。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [257] [PatTree: a novel approach for automated creation of multimodal, graph-based patient representations for medical classification tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02692)
*Julia Gehrmann,Lars Quakulinski,Hamza Naseem,Oya Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PatTree，一种基于图的、可自动从真实世界临床数据中构建的多模态患者整体表示方法，无需预标准化输入即可实现早期数据整合，并在ADNI-1数据集（n=763）的三分类任务中达到98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据能提升医疗AI分类性能，但临床真实数据的异质性（缺失值、多时间点、多模态、格式和语义不一致）给结构化分析和AI应用带来巨大挑战。现有的数据协调方法资源密集且易出错，限制了可扩展性和可复现性。

Method: PatTree是一种基于图的患者整体表示方法，通过自动化结构化多模态临床数据实现早期数据整合，将异构临床数据统一表示为知识图谱，保留跨模态和数据源之间的语义关系，不依赖预标准化输入。

Result: 使用ADNI-1队列子集（n=763），在区分阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍和认知正常个体的三分类任务中，在保留测试集上达到98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数，达到最先进的分类性能。

Conclusion: 无假设的自动化多模态医疗数据结构化可以作为临床AI管道的可扩展基础，绕过繁琐的数据准备和标准化步骤，同时保持最先进的分类性能。

Abstract: Access to holistic, multimodal data improves the performance of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in medical classification tasks compared to utilizing single modalities or data sources. However, the inherent heterogeneity and complexity of clinical real-world data pose significant challenges to structured data analysis and AI application. This heterogeneity includes missing values, multiple time points, diverse modalities, and inconsistent formats and semantics. Data harmonization prior to data integration tackles this challenge but remains resource-intensive and error-prone, limiting the scalability and reproducibility of holistic, AI-driven decision support on clinical real-world data. We therefore propose PatTree, a graph-based, holistic representation of patients that can be derived from real-world clinical data through the automated structuring of multimodal clinical data. PatTree enables early-stage data integration without relying on pre-standardized inputs. While representing heterogeneous clinical data within a unified knowledge graph, PatTree preserves the semantic relationships between data elements across modalities and data sources, facilitating interoperability and machine-interpretable data access. Using a subset of the ADNI-1 cohort (n = 763), we demonstrate that classification of patients is directly feasible on PatTree reaching state-of-the-art classification performance. In the three-class classification task distinguishing Alzheimer's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and cognitively normal individuals, we achieve a balanced accuracy of 98.5% and an F$_1$ score of 0.987 on the held-out test set. Our results show that assumption-free, automated structuring of multimodal medical data can serve as a scalable foundation for clinical AI pipelines bypassing tedious data preparation and standardization.

</details>


### [258] [Measuring Explainer Stability via Attribution Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02697)
*Eddie Conti,Álvaro Parafita,Axel Brando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分布的分析框架，用于捕捉归因方法（AMs）得分的不稳定性，衡量排名归因向量的可分离程度，并确定特征排名可靠的最大索引，同时将该框架扩展到跨数据集比较不同归因方法排名的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归因方法由于定义中的随机成分，可能产生不稳定的归因分数，这影响了黑盒模型解释的可靠性，而现有方法缺乏系统性的框架来评估这种稳定性。

Method: 提出基于分布的框架来表征归因分数的稳定性，通过分析排名归因向量的可分离程度，计算特征排名保持可靠的最大索引，并将该框架扩展到跨数据集比较不同归因方法的排名稳健性。

Result: 通过实验展示了如何应用该方法评估解释器的稳定性，验证了所提框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为评估归因方法的稳定性提供了一个补充性标准，有助于更好地理解和选择可靠的黑盒模型解释工具。

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

</details>


### [259] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NANQ是一个面向模拟存算一体（CIM）的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，通过将硬件噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度并识别各层精度饱和点来分配位宽，在eFlash CIM SoC上显著提升了量化模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟CIM虽能实现高能效神经网络推理，但器件变异和读取噪声会严重损害低比特量化模型的性能。现有CIM量化方法仅最小化理想量化误差，忽略硬件噪声底限，导致精度分配效率低下。

Method: NANQ首先从eFlash CIM阵列的实测响应中建模幅值相关的权重噪声，将噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度——对低噪声区域分配更细分辨率，在高噪声区域避免无效精度。随后通过统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，据此分配逐层位宽，实现混合精度非均匀量化。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上，2比特权重幅值量化下，NANQ使视觉模型精度平均提升8.05个百分点，语言模型困惑度（PPL）平均降低54.7%（对比PowerQuant）。混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可获得额外量化资源的大部分收益。

Conclusion: NANQ证明了将硬件噪声纳入量化设计能有效克服CIM的精度瓶颈，以低比特实现接近高精度量化的性能，为模拟存算一体化芯片的实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [260] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在比较随机训练模型时，如何利用训练日志（training logs）作为协变量进行arm-specific调整，以减少模型性能比较的不确定性。实验发现在视觉任务（3种架构、3个数据集）中，基于早期训练日志的简单调整通常能降低比较的不确定性，但协变量选择的搜索会引入噪声，限制其收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较随机训练模型需要从多次运行中估计性能差异及其不确定性，本文探索能否利用同一批运行的训练日志作为协变量，使模型比较更加精确。

Method: 采用arm-specific协变量调整方法：每个模型仅使用自身运行的统计数据进行调整，原始均值差异仍作为报告的效果量。在视觉任务中涵盖3种架构和3个数据集，评估基于早期训练日志的简单调整对比较不确定性的影响。

Result: 基于早期训练日志的简单调整通常能减少模型比较的不确定性；但广泛搜索日志池中相关性最高的统计量会引入过多噪声，反而适得其反，即使事后存在有用的统计量。

Conclusion: 训练日志可用于提高模型比较的精确度，但前提是调整过程需避免较大的选择噪声，协变量选择是主要限制因素。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [261] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可寻址且保持基数的虚拟内存机制，通过将全局内存分解为独立的可读写状态，并将每个槽位查询作为私有键/值锚点，解决了消息传递神经网络中虚拟节点标准管线的有限容量瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准虚拟节点通过VN管线将图压缩为单一同质状态并广播给所有节点，存在有限的容量瓶颈。作者希望在不使用自注意力的前提下，利用辅助虚拟内存来缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: 基于Two-Radius分析，提出两个关键要求：(1) 将全局内存分解为独立可写可读的状态，通过可寻址交叉注意力槽实现；(2) 解决softmax注意力对均匀复制的不变性导致的多重性丢失问题，通过将每个槽位查询作为私有键/值锚点来恢复归一化质量，从而在有限颜色域上实现单射多重集表示和1-WL细化。

Result: 在多重性感知的Two-Radius、子图计数和约束链接集预测等实验上验证了该方法的有效性，计算复杂度为O(nMd)。

Conclusion: 可寻址且保持基数的虚拟内存能够在无自注意力条件下有效缓解虚拟节点的容量瓶颈，并支持1-WL级别的表达能力。

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [262] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部收敛输入神经网络（NNLCI）的新方法，用于提升多资产期权定价数值方法的效率。NNLCI利用神经网络对粗网格和细网格数值解进行局部校正，仅需少量高保真训练数据，在Black-Scholes方程（1-3维）和Heston随机波动率模型下，能将细网格解的均方根误差降低约4-12倍，显著降低高维问题的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多资产期权定价中的高维数值方法计算成本高昂，实时交易和风险管理场景下对计算效率有迫切需求，因此需要一种既能保持精度又能显著降低计算开销的方法。

Method: 采用神经网络对粗网格与细网格数值解的差异进行局部校正，引入最简洁的NNLCI输入格式，仅需少量高保真训练数据即可完成训练。在1-3维Black-Scholes方程下的现金或无现金期权、以及Heston模型下的单资产向下敲出障碍看涨期权上进行验证。

Result: 在各类测试场景中，NNLCI将细网格数值解的均方根误差（RMSE）降低了约4-12倍，且仅在少量参数组合子集上训练即可实现，展现出低训练成本与强泛化能力。

Conclusion: NNLCI显著降低了高维期权定价问题的计算需求，适用于实时期权交易和风险管理，具有训练成本低、泛化能力强的优势。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [263] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"评估盲区"（evaluation blindness）概念：测量函数在系统实际失败时仍产生与健康状态无法区分的读数，且无辅助信号提示异常。该问题在训练阶段（奖励模型被博弈、重要性采样计算错误、基准污染）和部署阶段（六类生产失败，其中操作类100%静默）均出现。作者提供了统一的检测谓词、四例训练时案例研究、基于50起真实事件的六类分类法（53%的公开失败是静默的），以及失败预算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失败，且失败会通过训练循环、评估管道和生产监控栈传播，直到下游损害使其可见。已有文献将训练时和部署时的失败检测分开处理，缺乏统一的正式框架来理解和检测这种静默失败问题。

Method: 1) 形式化定义评估盲区与可检测性谓词，统一训练与部署两个生命周期阶段；2) 提供四例训练时案例研究，包括TRL PR #6594中的真实实现bug；3) 构建六类失败分类法，基于50起来自法院文件和监管文件的真实事件验证；4) 提出失败预算框架，将可接受的失败率与用例风险等级挂钩。

Result: 在50起可验证的公开失败事件中，53%是静默失败。六类生产失败分类法中，操作类（Operational）失败按结构定义100%静默。TRL PR #6594显示即使在loss正常下降时梯度也可能被破坏。

Conclusion: 测量基础设施是贯穿整个AI生命周期的正确性关注点，而不仅仅是评估阶段的问题。数据、代码和分类法模式已在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [264] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐层持久同调分析，研究了预测编码网络（PCN）中学习表示的拓扑结构。实验发现：较小的PCN跨层早期比大模型更早地折叠连通分量（Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]）；简化发生的深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58）；与匹配的MLP相比，PCN平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量。结果表明持久同调为PCN中压缩-重建权衡提供了量化视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预测编码网络中学习表示的拓扑结构如何随层变化，以及模型容量和双向循环动力学如何影响压缩与重建之间的权衡。

Method: 对在合成分类数据集（测试准确率≥99.9%）和MNIST（测试准确率≥95%）上训练的高性能PCN，使用逐层持久同调分析量化不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征的跨层变化，并进行种子级bootstrap比较。

Result: 1) 较小PCN跨层早期比大模型更早折叠连通分量（Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]）；2) 简化深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58），即简化越晚的架构重建越好；3) PCN比匹配的MLP平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量。

Conclusion: 持久同调为PCN的压缩-重建权衡提供了有效的量化分析工具；模型容量和预测编码推理的循环双向动力学共同决定了这一权衡在层间的解决时机。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [265] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了预训练大模型向小模型"子克隆"（subcloning）转换的可能性，以Pythia家族1.4B→410M为案例，发现表示层对齐强而参数层对齐弱，转换价值主要存在于初始化而非权重投影；通过最小二乘补偿和方差保持重缩放两个独立杠杆，在低预算下优于其他变体，高预算下与最强基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型家族通常每个规模都从头训练，但能否将预训练大模型转换为更小的"兄弟"模型是一个尚未被充分探索的问题。本文旨在系统性地回答这一转换是否可行、转换的价值来源以及最优转换策略。

Method: 以Pythia家族1.4B→410M为端到端案例，采用以下方法：(1)通过岭回归分析表示层和参数层对齐程度；(2)测试密集权重投影的功能性破坏，验证其不是装配伪影；(3)用混洗对照检验权重残差的统计显著性；(4)在匹配预算的持续预训练中，分解出两个独立杠杆——最小二乘补偿（优化零样本性能）和方差保持重缩放（优化训练动力学）；(5)在更宽和更深的缩减对、以及6.9B→1.4B更大尺度上绘制方法边界。

Result: (1)表示层对齐强（R²=0.84）而参数层对齐弱；(2)直接权重投影因破坏旋转、注意力头、GELU和LayerNorm结构而功能上具有破坏性；(3)最佳线性算子后的权重残差在统计上与噪声无异；(4)转换价值主要存在于初始化；(5)最小二乘补偿在30M token低预算下优于最强的子克隆变体（宽度缩减对：84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7，深度缩减对：109.3 vs 117.9），33倍更大预算时两者收敛至持平；(6)在6.9B→1.4B尺度上两个杠杆叠加会过度校正，归因于补偿求解在大宽度下的病态条件。

Conclusion: 预训练模型到更小模型的转换在低预算场景下是token高效的，转换初始化始终优于从头训练（低预算时优势高达18倍），但在更大预算下优势收窄。转换的边界在于尺度——当捐赠模型约为目标模型5倍大小时需要维度感知正则化来修复病态求解问题。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [266] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后置的概念漂移缓解方法，通过对基模型在不同训练周期上的参数变化进行低秩分解和阻尼外推，适用于树模型、神经网络和表格基础模型等多种架构，在Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上超越了所有基线系列，且仅需数秒训练，性能可与需要约1300 GPU小时预训练的SOTA方法匹敌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格模型在特征分布变化（数据漂移）或特征与目标关系变化（概念漂移）时性能会下降，而实时缓解这些问题很困难——因为标签数据可能无法即时获取，或重新训练模型不切实际。现有漂移缓解工具通常专用于神经网络架构，且需要修改训练方式。因此需要一种即插即用、适用于多种模型的后置方法。

Method: 算法流程：(1) 在每个带标签的训练周期上分别拟合基模型；(2) 以跨所有周期池化的单一锚点模型为参照，测量模型参数如何演化；(3) 用低秩因子分解压缩参数变化；(4) 对每个潜在因子使用阻尼正则化预测进行前向外推，从而预测未来时间点的模型参数以缓解概念漂移。

Result: 在18数据集Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，按其自身协议和指标评估，该方法改善了所应用到的每个基模型系列的性能，且仅需数秒训练即达到与SOTA方法Drift-Resilient TabPFN竞争的性能水平。相比之下，后者需要在大约1300个GPU小时中预训练数百万个合成数据集，且推理速度慢多个数量级。

Conclusion: 该后置方法提供了一种轻量级、模型无关的概念漂移缓解方案，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了训练和推理成本，尤其适用于需要在高精度、有限推理时间和模型大小约束下进行模型选择的场景。文章还探讨了将此方法扩展到其他模态的前景与挑战。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>


### [267] [Adaptive Sampling for Automated Post-Disaster Rapid Damage Assessment via Level-Set Cost-Aware Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02868)
*Boyang Xu,Mostafa Reisi Gahrooei,Mohammad Ilbeigi,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合成本感知贝叶斯优化与水平集估计的框架，用于持续引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）在灾后环境中高效评估建筑损伤，在降低运行成本的同时减小预测不确定性，并支持快速应急响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灾后损伤评估方法依赖静态、劳动密集型的数据采集策略，成本高昂且难以适应动态灾后环境，亟需一种快速、可靠且经济高效的自动化评估方法。

Method: 提出成本感知贝叶斯优化与水平集估计相结合的框架，动态引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）前往信息量最丰富的区域，实时更新不同地理区域的损伤估计，在减小不确定性的同时最小化运行成本。

Result: 在受控合成玩具研究中验证了框架能高效追踪损伤边界、恢复底层损伤图并快速降低预测不确定性；在使用R2D软件生成的高保真灾害数据上，算法能够提供准确且及时的损伤估计。

Conclusion: 该框架能够提供准确及时的灾后损伤评估，有效支持信息充分且快速的应急响应行动。

Abstract: Natural disasters frequently inflict severe damage to the built environment, which demands a rapid, reliable, and cost-effective damage assessment for emergency response. However, traditional methods for post-disaster damage assessment often rely on static, labor-intensive data collection strategies that can be prohibitively expensive and struggle to adapt to dynamic post-disaster conditions. In this study, we propose a cost-aware Bayesian optimization framework combined with level-set estimation that continuously guides autonomous data collectors, e.g., an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), toward the most informative regions. By dynamically updating damage estimates across different geographic zones, our approach systematically reduces uncertainty while minimizing operational costs. The proposed framework is first validated using a controlled synthetic toy study, demonstrating the agent's ability to efficiently trace damage boundaries, recover the underlying damage map, and rapidly reduce predictive uncertainty. Furthermore, the approach is evaluated using high-fidelity disaster data generated by the Regional Resilience Determination (R2D) software. The results of the algorithm provide accurate and timely damage estimates that support informative and fast emergency response.

</details>


### [268] [Contrast-invariant deep ptychography neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02869)
*Albert Vong,Steven Henke,Oliver Hoidn,Hanna Ruth,Junjing Deng,Apurva Mehta,David Shapiro,Alexander Hexemer,Nicholas Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Ptychography神经网络在分布外泛化时的尺度不一致问题，提出因子分解策略，将学习到的物体纹理与测量尺度解耦，使单一训练网络能跨不同照明条件生成一致的重建结果，并在5个实验数据集上将Fourier误差降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ptychography神经网络在分布外泛化时存在尺度不一致问题，限制了其在真实世界场景中的实际应用。

Method: 采用因子分解策略将物体纹理与测量尺度解耦；预测实部和虚部而非传统的振幅和相位表示；引入合成物体采样策略以最小化合成训练数据与实验目标之间的相位分布差异。

Result: 与之前的PtychoPINN-torch基线相比，在覆盖多个光束线和设施的5个实验数据集上，Fourier误差最多降低5倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效解决了Ptychography神经网络的尺度泛化瓶颈，显著提升了跨实验条件下的重建一致性，增强了其在实际应用中的可行性与稳健性。

Abstract: Ptychography neural networks suffer from scaling inconsistencies when generalizing out of distribution, limiting their real world viability. We address this scaling mismatch using a factorization strategy which decouples the learned object texture from measurement scaling, enabling a single trained network to produce measurement-consistent reconstructions across varying illumination conditions. This requires predicting the learned object in real and imaginary units instead of the canonical amplitude and phase representation. We additionally introduce a synthetic object sampling strategy that minimizes phase distribution mismatch between synthetic training data and experimental targets. These improvements yield up to a 5x reduction in Fourier error over the previous PtychoPINN-torch baseline across 5 experimental datasets spanning multiple beamlines and facilities.

</details>


### [269] [Maglev: Sliding Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02870)
*Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\ours{}的循环Transformer架构，它带有固定大小的记忆，能够泛化滑动窗口注意力同时保持训练时的可并行性。该架构由两个耦合模型组成：一个利用全注意力的预填充器Q用于生成记忆目标，以及一个仅使用滑动窗口注意力和循环K/V注入的解码器P用于下一词预测。通过记忆一致性损失对齐两者的记忆，推理时可仅使用P。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的滑动窗口注意力虽然高效但缺乏对长距离历史信息的有效利用，而传统的循环Transformer在记忆建模上存在不足。作者希望设计一种既能保持训练并行性、又能高效利用长距离上下文的架构。

Method: 提出一个由预填充器Q和解码器P组成的双模型架构。Q使用全注意力（实践中穿插使用全注意力和滑动窗口注意力）生成记忆目标m'_t；P仅使用滑动窗口注意力和循环K/V注入生成解码器记忆m_t。通过记忆一致性损失使m_t与m'_t对齐，使推理时仅需使用P。还探索了P和Q之间的参数共享以降低参数开销。

Result: 实验表明\ours{}在验证损失和下游预训练基准上均优于滑动窗口注意力和潜变量循环Transformer基线。此外，在P和Q之间共享参数可以在保留大部分性能收益的同时减少参数存储。

Conclusion: \ours{}通过预填充器与解码器的耦合训练和记忆一致性约束，实现了兼具训练可并行性和推理时高效长距离上下文利用的循环注意力架构，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: We introduce \ours{}, a recurrent Transformer architecture with fixed-size memory that generalizes sliding-window attention while remaining parallelizable during training. \ours{} consists of two coupled models: a prefiller $Q$, which leverages full attention\footnote{In practice, we use interleaved full and sliding-window attention for $Q$, as this yields stronger performance. The essential requirement is that $Q$ be more expressive than $P$, with access to the full history.} to produce memory targets $m'_t$, and a decoder $P$, which uses only sliding-window attention and recurrent K/V injection to produce decoder memories $m_t$ for next-token prediction. We train \ours{} with a memory consistency loss that aligns $m_t$ with $m'_t$, allowing inference to use $P$ alone. Empirically, \ours{} improves validation loss and downstream pretraining benchmarks over sliding-window and latent recurrent transformer baselines. Moreover, sharing parameters between $P$ and $Q$ reduces parameter memory while preserving most of the gains.

</details>


### [270] [GoT-CD: Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery and the Fragility of Post-hoc Path-Specific Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02877)
*Nitish Nagesh,Elahe Khatibi,Thomas Dean Hughes,Mahdi Bagheri,Pratik Gajane,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GoT-CD方法，将完整候选边集作为推理单元进行因果发现，在所有基准上返回有效DAG，但在结构保真度之外，路径特定的公平性审计仍可能因发现图中缺失关键路径而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现方法在临床应用日益广泛，用于支持机制推理和公平性审计。然而，路径特定反事实公平性依赖给定的因果图，发现阶段的错误会被继承。现有评估仅基于聚合结构指标，没有评估审计所依赖的特定路径是否在发现结果中幸存，导致公平性审计可能因路径缺失而失效。

Method: GoT-CD将推理单元从单个图升级为完整候选边集，并行生成多个图，通过确定性有效性函数评分，在硬联合约束（禁止发明边）下合并，并通过贪婪投影确保无环性后才提交。该方法利用全图Graph-of-Thoughts推理生成无环发现图。

Result: GoT-CD在五个基准上均返回有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory数据集上取得LLM方法中最佳的DAG有效F1分数。在含已知不公平路径的Alzheimer's基准上，八个发现图中五个无法恢复从敏感属性到结果的路径，导致报告零总体效应而中介效应仍然存在。

Conclusion: 结构保真度不能保证公平性保真度，即使发现图在结构指标上表现良好，路径特定的公平性审计仍可能因关键路径缺失而失效。因此，下游路径特定的公平性分析必须与结构发现并行进行。

Abstract: Causal discovery recovers directed structure from observational data and is increasingly used in clinical settings to support mechanism reasoning and fairness audits of predictive models. Path-specific counterfactual fairness asks whether a protected attribute influences an outcome through illegitimate pathways, but these estimands are defined relative to a supplied causal graph and therefore inherit whatever errors the discovery step introduces. Discovery methods are routinely scored on aggregate structural metrics that weight all edges equally, and no established evaluation asks whether the specific pathway an audit depends on survives discovery---or what the audit reports when that pathway is missing. Here we show that full-graph Graph-of-Thoughts reasoning yields acyclic discovered graphs that are structurally competitive with large language model (LLM) baselines, yet that structural fidelity alone does not guarantee fairness-faithful audits.
