We introduce GoT-CD, in which the reasoning unit is a complete candidate edge set: multiple graphs are generated in parallel, scored by a deterministic validity function, and merged under a hard union constraint that forbids invented edges, with greedy projection enforcing a DAG before commitment. GoT-CD returns a valid DAG on all five reported benchmarks and achieves the best DAG-valid F1 score among LLM methods on Asia, Alzheimer's, and COVID-Respiratory datasets. On an Alzheimer's benchmark with known unfair path, a post-hoc path-specific audit shows that five of eight discovered graphs recover no path from the sensitive attribute to the outcome and therefore report a null overall effect while mediated effects persist, necessitating downstream path-specific fairness analysis along with structural discovery.

</details>


### [271] [Population-Robust Feature Selection via Generalized Welfare Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02887)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PopFS是一种用于多群体场景的鲁棒特征选择方法。它学习一个共享的可部署特征集，同时允许每个群体训练自己的模型，通过可调福利目标在整体预测收益与最弱势群体保护之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征选择方法通常针对单一大型群体优化，而鲁棒方法则倾向于为所有群体学习一个共享模型。然而实际部署中，同一个有限的特征集（如问卷、测试面板或传感器）往往需要服务于多个异质群体，现有的两种方法都无法很好地兼顾群体差异与共享特征集的部署需求。

Method: PopFS首先使用多任务稀疏学习来缩减候选特征池，然后通过对候选特征进行排序（包括添加和交换操作）直接搜索硬特征集，仅对排名靠前的候选进行完整的模型重训练。其核心目标采用可调福利函数，使从业者能够在整体预测收益与对获益最少群体的更强保护之间进行权衡。

Result: 在来自五个表格及公共卫生数据集的六个预测任务构成的八个人口划分上，PopFS在平均性能和最差群体性能方面均表现优异，并能扩展到数千个候选特征。一项43个州的COVID-19即时预测研究进一步表明，调整福利目标可以在平均性能几乎不变的情况下改善最不受益群体的表现，且所选症状信号产生可解释的变化。

Conclusion: PopFS为跨异质群体的共享特征集选择提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案，在公平性与整体性能之间提供了灵活权衡，并在大规模真实数据（如COVID-19各州预测）中验证了其实用性和可解释性。

Abstract: Choosing which features to collect is a deployment decision: the same limited questionnaire, test panel, or sensor set may need to serve several heterogeneous populations. Standard feature-selection methods typically optimize for one large population, while existing robust approaches tend to learn one shared model for every population. We introduce PopFS, a method for learning one shared, deployable feature set that is robust to population differences while letting each pop- ulation train its own model. PopFS uses a tunable welfare objective that lets practitioners balance overall predictive ben- efit against stronger protection of the populations that benefit least. To make this objective practical at scale, PopFS first uses multitask sparse learning to reduce the candidate pool, then searches directly over hard feature sets by ranking promising additions and swaps and fully refitting only a shortlist. Across eight population splits from six prediction tasks drawn from five tabular and public-health datasets, PopFS consistently achieves strong average and worst-population performance while scaling to thousands of candidate features. A 43-state COVID-19 nowcasting study further shows that changing the welfare objective can improve the least-served states with lit- tle change in average performance and yields an interpretable change in the selected symptom signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/PopFS.

</details>


### [272] [Robust Counterfactual Policy Optimisation via Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02893)
*Jessica Lally,Milad Kazemi,Nicola Paoletti,David Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）固有的随机性，形式化了概率非确定性因果模型下的反事实策略优化问题，将潜在混淆因子与不可约随机性正确分离，并提出了一种在敏感性分析框架下识别鲁棒反事实策略的首个实用优化方法，并在脓毒症治疗模拟器上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实推断方法通常假设确定性因果模型，所有随机性均来自潜在变量，这与MDP本质上具有随机性的特点不符。因此需要一种能够正确处理概率非确定性因果模型的方法，将潜在混淆与随机性分离，以更好地进行反事实策略优化。

Method: 形式化概率非确定性因果模型下的反事实策略优化问题，在敏感性分析框架下提出鲁棒反事实策略识别方法，将潜在混淆因子与不可约随机性正确分离。

Result: 在脓毒症治疗模拟器上进行了验证，其中糖尿病状态作为隐藏的全局混淆因子，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文是首个在概率非确定性因果模型下提出实用反事实策略优化问题的工作，能够在敏感性分析框架下识别鲁棒的反事实策略，适用于具有随机性和隐藏混淆的真实序列决策场景。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference approaches for sequential decision-making typically assume deterministic causal models, where all randomness stems from latent variables. However, Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are inherently stochastic. We address this by formalising counterfactual policy optimisation under probabilistic nondeterministic causal models, which properly separates latent confounding from irreducible stochasticity, and here propose a first practical optimisation problem for identifying robust counterfactual policies under a sensitivity analysis framework. We validate our approach on a sepsis treatment simulator, where diabetes status acts as a hidden global confounder.

</details>


### [273] [AnchorKV: Anchor-Residual KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02901)
*Malik Khalaf,Yara Shamshoum,Nitzan Hodos,Yuval Sieradzki,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种新的KV缓存压缩方案，通过将缓存表示为少量精确存储的锚点，并用每个token与其最相似锚点的关系来表达其他token，实现了20倍压缩且不丢弃任何token，在70B规模下保留了99%的全缓存性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中，KV缓存是主要内存瓶颈。现有的两类方法各有缺陷：逐出方法永久丢弃token，可能导致后续需要时性能下降；量化方法虽保留所有token但压缩率有限。

Method: AnchorKV采用锚点压缩机制：用一小部分精确存储的锚点来表示整个缓存，每个非锚点token通过与其最相似锚点的关系来表达，仅对模型输出影响最大的近似token进行精细化优化。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上一致保持准确性，70B规模下保留了全缓存得分的99%，同时实现了20倍的缓存压缩率，且不丢弃任何token。

Conclusion: AnchorKV在保留所有token信息的前提下实现了大幅度的缓存压缩，兼顾了高压缩率和准确性，为长上下文LLM推理的内存瓶颈提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the primary memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference. Existing approaches attack it from opposite ends: eviction methods permanently discard tokens, degrading performance whenever a discarded token later proves essential, while quantization methods retain all tokens at low precision but offer limited compression. We propose AnchorKV, a compression scheme that shrinks the cache by $20\times$ without discarding a single token. AnchorKV represents the cache using a small set of anchors stored exactly, expresses every other token through its most similar anchor, and refines only those whose approximation most affects the model's output. AnchorKV consistently preserves accuracy across models and datasets, retaining 99% of the full-cache score at the 70B scale, while keeping the entire context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [274] [Bayesian Data Reweighting Improves Multimodal Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02907)
*Jingchen Sun,Shaobo Han,Ruiyi Zhang,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Ming Liu,Yitao Long,Vishnu Suresh Lokhande,Changyou Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯数据重加权框架，将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量，通过共轭先验下的闭式后验更新和随机EM优化，自适应地降低假负样本权重，在三个检索器和七个基于知识的VQA基准上一致提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比训练方法将所有不匹配的查询-文档对视为等信息量的负样本，但许多不匹配文档实际上语义相关或部分有用（即假负样本），这损害了多模态检索器的训练效果。

Method: 提出贝叶斯数据重加权（Bayesian Data Reweighting）框架：将查询-文档重要性建模为潜变量，利用共轭先验获得闭式后验更新，并采用随机EM优化自适应推断后验权重，从而降低假负样本的权重。

Result: 在三个不同的检索器和七个基于知识的VQA基准上，一致性地提升了检索准确率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据重加权是一种有效且通用的方法，能够通过概率框架自动识别并降低假负样本的影响，显著改善多模态检索器的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal retrievers are essential for knowledge-based visual question answering, where they retrieve external evidence for image-question pairs. However, existing contrastive training methods typically treat all unmatched query-document pairs as equally informative negatives, which is problematic because many unmatched documents may still be semantically relevant or partially useful. We propose Bayesian Data Reweighting, a probabilistic framework that models query-document importance as latent variables and adaptively infers posterior weights to downweight likely false negatives. With closed-form posterior updates under conjugate priors and stochastic EM optimization, our method consistently improves retrieval accuracy across three retrievers and seven knowledge-based VQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [275] [Forecasting Revenue with its Customer-Base Drivers: When and Why Coordination Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02911)
*Kyeongbin Kim,Daniel McCarthy,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出客户基础多任务Transformer（CBMT）模型，利用966家公司在25个行业的周度交易面板数据，通过共享结构学习、保留独立的原始预测并将其组合与下游收入对齐，实现客户基础驱动的营收预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总收入预测无法揭示变化源于客户获取、重复购买、每单支出还是相互抵消的变动，需要更细粒度的客户基础预测来支持收购预算、需求规划和客户价值评估。

Method: 开发Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer（CBMT），学习跨公司/行业的共享结构，同时保留各原始指标（获取、重复购买、每单支出）的独立预测，并通过对齐机制使组合预测与下游总收入一致。

Result: CBMT平均总销售额误差比最强基准低30%；比直接预测总收入的Transformer低2.65%（但配对差异不显著，p=.222）；对74.3%的公司优于独立单任务预测；在23/24个基准-结果比较中源头MAE更低。客户基础动态高度波动时所有模型精度下降，CBMT优势收窄；校准期路由规则不优于始终部署CBMT。

Conclusion: 协调的客户基础预测能有效支持营收规划，但其优势在客户动态高度不稳定时减弱，需要谨慎使用。

Abstract: Revenue forecasts guide acquisition budgets, demand planning, and customer-based valuations, yet an aggregate forecast does not show whether change reflects acquisition, repeat purchasing, spending per order, or offsetting movements. Using weekly transaction panels for 966 companies in 25 industries, the authors develop the Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer (CBMT), which learns shared structure, retains separate primitive forecasts, and aligns their combination with downstream revenue. CBMT's mean total-sales error is 30% below the strongest representative established customer-base benchmark. It is also 2.65% below a Transformer that forecasts total sales directly, although the paired difference is not statistically significant (p=.222), and it beats separately estimated single-task forecasts for 74.3% of firms. CBMT's source MAE is lower in 23 of 24 benchmark-by-outcome comparisons, with the remaining difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. Firms whose primitives co-move more strongly are more likely to benefit from joint forecasting; selected-family scenario-3 comparisons are consistent with gains from shared representation and revenue alignment but remain diagnostic rather than causal. Accuracy deteriorates for all models when customer-base dynamics are highly volatile, and CBMT's advantage narrows there. Calibration-period routing rules do not improve average accuracy over always deploying CBMT. The results show how coordinated customer-base forecasts support revenue planning and when they warrant greater caution.

</details>


### [276] [When Should Graph Attention Be Sparse? Learning a Per-Edge Tsallis Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02938)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LTGA（可学习Tsallis图注意力），通过在图注意力中引入可学习的Tsallis熵指数q，使其能够从重尾（q<1）到softmax（q=1）再到紧凑支撑（q>1）的注意力形状之间连续插值。实验表明LTGA-Edge在8个基准上取得最佳平均排名（2.75），但统计检验不显著，且学习q的效果并不优于网格搜索q的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同质图和异质图需要不同的注意力形状，而传统的softmax归一化（基于Shannon熵）只能提供一种固定的注意力分布，无法同时满足两类图的需求。

Method: 提出LTGA图注意力层，将Tsallis熵指数q作为可学习参数与网络权重联合训练，支持从全局标量到逐边索引的四种粒度，并通过有界重参数化确保模型从GAT基线开始训练。

Result: 在8个基准、10个种子上，LTGA-Edge平均排名2.75最优，但omnibus检验p=0.199不显著。学习q的准确率（61.7%）略低于验证集调优的冻结网格（61.4%）、调优α-entmax（62.2%）和容量匹配的q≡1对照组（62.0%）。但学习q的优势在于单次运行而非网格搜索，且可解释：q偏离1时剪枝42%的注意力系数为零，且被剪枝的是错误边，恢复它们需7.1分代价，而随机剪枝同比例需13.0分更多代价。

Conclusion: 学习q的主要价值不在于提升准确率（与搜索或固定基线相当），而在于避免网格搜索的单次运行效率和提供可解释的注意力剪枝机制——能识别并剪除错误的边，这比随机剪枝更精确有效。

Abstract: Graph attention normalizes neighborhood scores with softmax, the maximum-entropy choice under Shannon statistics. But homophilic and heterophilic graphs want different attention shapes, and one fixed normalization cannot serve both. We propose \textbf{LTGA} (\textbf{L}earnable \textbf{T}sallis \textbf{G}raph \textbf{A}ttention), a graph attention layer whose Tsallis entropic index $q$ is learned jointly with the weights, interpolating continuously between heavy-tailed ($q\!<\!1$), softmax ($q\!=\!1$) and compact-support ($q\!>\!1$) attention at four granularities from a global scalar to a per-edge index, under a bounded reparameterization that starts every model at the GAT baseline. Across eight benchmarks at ten seeds, LTGA-Edge takes the best average rank ($2.75$), but the omnibus test does not reject ($p\!=\!0.199$) and learning $q$ does not beat searching it: a validation-tuned frozen grid reaches $61.4\%$, tuned $α$-entmax $62.2\%$ and a capacity-matched $q\!\equiv\!1$ control $62.0\%$, against $61.7\%$ for LTGA-Edge. What the learned index buys is one run instead of a grid, and an interpretable mechanism: where $q$ leaves $1$, it prunes $42\%$ of attention coefficients to exactly zero, and those edges are selectively the wrong ones, restoring them costs $7.1$ points, while random pruning at the same rate costs $13.0$ more. Project page: https://kleyt0n.github.io/ltga

</details>


### [277] [Federated generative event models for tokenized electronic health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02939)
*Michael C. Burkhart,Luke Solo,Inhyeok Lee,S'Khaja Charles,Zewei "Whiskey" Liao,Kaveri Chhikara,Dema Therese,Wan-Ting Liao,Catherine A. Gao,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了电子健康记录基础模型在跨机构场景下的联邦训练。作者使用来自三个独立医疗系统的122,251次ICU住院数据（统一为Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format），构建了基于标记化的生成式事件模型（GEMs），并在12个临床预测任务上比较了站点内、跨站点、集中式和联邦式训练配置。结果表明GEMs的跨站点性能损失显著小于传统监督模型，联邦学习（FedAvg/FedAvgM）能接近集中式训练的性能，但集中式多站点训练的收益有限，仅在本地数据不足时价值明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录基础模型受到数据孤岛和跨站点迁移性能显著下降的制约，亟需评估联邦学习等隐私保护训练方法在这种场景下的可行性和有效性。

Method: 使用三个独立医疗系统的122,251次ICU住院数据（统一为CLIF格式），训练基于标记化的生成式事件模型（GEMs），在12个临床预测任务上比较四种训练配置：站点内训练、跨站点评估、集中式训练和联邦学习（FedAvg和FedAvgM）。

Result: GEMs在站点内和跨站点ROC-AUC上均表现最佳，跨站点性能损失（0.025 ROC-AUC和0.027 PR-AUC）远小于LightGBM（0.079和0.089）。联邦学习接近集中式训练性能，5-10轮通信即可获得大部分收益。但集中式多站点训练相对完全本地训练仅提供小幅提升，且仅在本地数据有限时优势明显。

Conclusion: 联邦GEM训练在技术上可行且能保留大部分集中式训练性能，但核心挑战在于学习可迁移的表征，以便将多个医疗系统中更大但异质的数据转化为可靠的目标站点收益。

Abstract: Electronic health record foundation models are limited by institutionally siloed data and substantial performance degradation under cross-site transfer. We evaluated federated training of tokenized generative event models (GEMs) across 122,251 intensive care hospitalizations from three independent health systems harmonized to the Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format. Models were assessed on 12 post-24-hour clinical prediction tasks using within-site, cross-site, centralized, and federated training configurations. GEMs achieved the highest mean within-site and cross-site ROC-AUC and were substantially more transportable than conventional supervised models: their average cross-site penalties were 0.025 ROC-AUC and 0.027 PR-AUC, compared with 0.079 and 0.089 for LightGBM. Federated Learning (FedAvg and FedAvgM) approached the performance of centralized GEM training, with most gains obtained within 5-10 communication rounds. However, centralized multi-site training provided only modest improvements over complete local training. Multi-site models were most useful when local training data were limited, with their advantage narrowing as institutional data accumulated. These findings show that federated GEM training is technically feasible and preserves most centralized performance, but that the main open challenge is learning transportable representations to translate larger, but heterogeneous data from multiple health systems into a reliable target-site benefit.

</details>


### [278] [Sedentary Behavior Classification for Wearable Sensors with a CNN-BiLSTM Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02946)
*Yuliang Chen,Weiwei Shi,Jingjing Zou,Rong Zablocki,Animesh Kumar,Jordan A. Carlson,Sheri J. Hartman,Mikael Anne Greenwood-Hickman,Paul R. Hibbing,Marta Jankowska,Jay Yang,Arun Kumar,Loki Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将基于髋部加速度计数据训练的深度学习模型（CHAP，CNN-BiLSTM架构）迁移到腕部加速度计数据进行坐姿与非坐姿分类。通过零样本评估和不同量级标注数据的微调实验，发现髋部预训练模型在腕部数据上性能下降，但微调后优于从头训练的Transformer模型，表明髋部预训练可作为腕部部署的有效起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 久坐行为的准确检测对研究长时间坐着相关的健康风险至关重要，但基于姿态的分类在可穿戴传感器上（尤其是腕部）仍然具有挑战性。研究者希望探索跨传感器位置（髋部到腕部）的模型迁移可行性。

Method: 使用CHAP模型（CNN-BiLSTM架构，最初为髋部加速度计开发），在iWatch数据集上进行实验。评估两种场景：1）零样本迁移——直接在腕部数据上测试髋部训练模型；2）微调迁移——使用不同数量的腕部标注数据对模型进行微调。数据集的真实姿态标签来自可穿戴相机。

Result: 髋部训练的模型在髋部数据上无需重训即可表现强劲，但在腕部数据上因传感器位置变化导致准确率下降。对CHAP进行微调相比于从头训练的Transformer模型具有一致的优势。

Conclusion: 髋部预训练为腕部部署提供了有用的起点，但仍需要进行针对腕部的适配以应对更高的信号变异性。

Abstract: Accurate detection of sedentary behavior is important for studying health risks related to prolonged sitting, but posture-based classification remains challenging with wearable sensors, especially at the wrist. We study whether a deep learning model trained on hip-worn accelerometer data can transfer to wrist-worn accelerometer data for sitting versus non-sitting classification. We use CHAP, a CNN-BiLSTM model originally developed for hip accelerometers, and evaluate its zero-shot performance on wrist data as well as its adaptation through finetuning with varying amounts of labeled wrist data. Experiments are conducted on the iWatch dataset with ground-truth posture labels derived from wearable cameras. The hip-trained model performs strongly on hip data without retraining, but accuracy drops on wrist data due to sensor placement shift. Finetuning CHAP provides consistent advantages over transformer models trained from scratch. These findings suggest that hip-based pretraining provides a useful starting point for wrist deployment, while highlighting the need for wrist-specific adaptation to handle higher signal variability.

</details>


### [279] [ATFlash: Per-RoPE-Wavelength Attention Windows for Compute/Memory-Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02947)
*Shun-ichiro Hayashi,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino,Takahiro Katagiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出按RoPE波长进行距离窗口剪枝的方法，将QK内积项中超过波长比例距离的项剪除。该方法剪枝率与输入无关，呈对数形式，可与动态稀疏方法正交叠加，在保持96-98% top-1匹配率和10^-3 nat级KL散度的同时，可剪除37-48%的QK内积项，并在长上下文下实现最高1.31倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE的注意力分数可精确分解为2D旋转频率对的求和，每个频率对的波长限制了其位置区分距离。现有的滑动窗口等方法忽略了RoPE的这一频率结构特性，而动态稀疏方法如MInference则依赖输入且缺乏确定性。

Method: 1) 基于RoPE频率分解结构，提出"per-RoPE-wavelength距离窗口"：按波长比例的距离阈值剪除QK内积项。2) 与滑动窗口不同，低频率对使每个key仍可达。3) 剪枝率具有封闭形式，随序列长度N呈对数增长。4) 与动态稀疏方法正交，可叠加使用。5) 实现为QK收缩轴的切片操作，不改变online-softmax递归，可便捷移植到FlashAttention-4和FlashInfer。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-3B上，本方法在原生上下文长度内剪除37-48%的QK内积项。top-1匹配率保持96-98%，输出分布KL散度在10^-3 nat级别。在RULER、OpenAI-MRCR、LongCodeQA和∞Bench等长上下文基准上绝对分数基本保持。在RTX PRO 6000上，Llama端口在128K上下文下实现最高1.29倍加速；Qwen2.5-7B-1M在1M上下文下剪除57%内积项，实现1.31倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法利用RoPE的频率结构实现了一种输入无关、确定性的高效注意力剪枝策略，既保持了输出质量，又在长上下文场景下实现了显著加速，且易于集成到现有FlashAttention等高效注意力实现中。

Abstract: The attention score with rotary position embeddings (RoPE) decomposes exactly into a sum over its 2D-rotation frequency pairs, and each pair's wavelength limits how far it can discriminate position. Aligned with this structure, we propose the per-RoPE-wavelength distance window: it prunes the query--key inner-product terms beyond a wavelength-proportional distance. Unlike a sliding window, every key remains reachable, at least through the low-frequency pairs. The reduction rate is input-independent, with a closed form logarithmic in the sequence length $N$, in contrast to dynamic-sparse methods like MInference. Such token-level selection is orthogonal to our frequency-level pruning. The window can therefore be applied on top of those methods. On Qwen2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-3B, the window prunes 37--48\% of the query--key inner-product terms within each model's native context length. Relative to full attention, the top-1 match rate stays at 96--98\% and the mean output-distribution KL at the $10^{-3}$-nat level on LongBench-v2 contexts. We examine absolute scores on long-context benchmarks such as RULER, OpenAI-MRCR, LongCodeQA, and $\infty$Bench: they are broadly preserved. We implement the window as a slice of the query--key contraction axis, leaving the online-softmax recurrences untouched, and port it with minimal diffs into the released FlashAttention-4 prefill and FlashInfer decode. On RTX PRO 6000 with Llama, both ports outpace stock with gains growing with context length, up to $1.29\times$ at 128K. End to end on Qwen2.5-7B-1M, with 57\% of the inner-product terms pruned, the speedup reaches $1.31\times$ at a 1M-token context.

</details>


### [280] [Rubrics as Privileged Information for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02948)
*Deepika Bablani,Ajay Gupta,Wanming Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）从可验证领域扩展到开放式生成任务，提出使用评分标准（rubrics）作为软特权信息（PI）进行蒸馏，相比硬参考完成PI和rubric作为奖励的强化学习（RaR RL）均表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）在数学等可验证领域效果良好，但依赖硬特权信息（如标准答案）。开放式生成任务缺乏唯一正确答案，需要探索如何将OPSD扩展到该领域。传统上rubric仅作为标量奖励用于RL，但作者认为其作为密集PI信号能提供更丰富的训练信息。

Method: 将OPSD从硬PI扩展到软PI，使用rubric作为密集特权信息指导蒸馏过程。rubric指定了一组有效响应共享的偏好结构，而非像参考完成那样过度约束到单一有效点。在Qwen和Llama模型家族上进行实验，使用HealthBench和RubricHub Science语料评估。

Result: Rubric作为PI（RuPI）在HealthBench上比RaR RL最多高出+0.10绝对分数；在匹配配方和KL方向下，比参考PI高出+0.034至+0.079绝对分数（跨越三个模型）。在RubricHub Science/RresearchQA上，软rubric PI优于参考PI蒸馏和RaR RL（66.6% vs 64.2%和57.6%）。

Conclusion: 在开放式生成任务中，软rubric PI比硬参考完成PI提供更大且更有效的训练信号，因为参考完成只是有效响应集合中的一个点，蒸馏向其会导致过度约束，而rubric编码了有效响应集合共享的偏好结构，更适合作为蒸馏信号。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a single model acts as both student and teacher with different contexts, has shown promise in verifiable domains like math, where hard privileged information (PI) in the form of ground-truth answers structurally constrains valid continuations. We extend OPSD to open-ended generation using soft PI in the form of rubrics that guide preferences but admit many valid responses. Rubrics have served as scalar rewards for reinforcement learning (RL); we show that they provide substantially richer signal as dense PI for distillation, and contrary to intuition, soft rubric PI provides a larger and more effective training signal on student roll-outs than hard reference completion PI in this regime. A reference completion is one point in a set of valid responses, so distilling towards it over-constrains the student, while rubrics specify the preference structure shared across the set of valid responses. We show the effectiveness of using rubrics as PI for open-ended generation across Qwen and Llama model families and show that it outperforms rubric-as-reward (RaR) RL using HealthBench, a benchmark that grades open-ended health responses against physician-created rubrics, providing dense token-level supervision for open-ended tasks; RuPI beats RaR RL by up to +0.10 absolute score and, under matched recipe and KL direction, beats reference-PI by +0.034 to +0.079 absolute score across three models. We further show that these findings generalize to training on the RubricHub Science corpus and evaluating on ResearchQA: soft rubric PI outperforms both reference-PI distillation and RaR RL (66.6% vs. 64.2% and 57.6%).

</details>


### [281] [Schedule-Informed Temporal Fusion Forecasting of Hourly Airport Security-Checkpoint Throughput](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02950)
*Yinxiao Zhang,Sen Wang,Yi Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将航班时刻表转换为安检吞吐量预测信号的框架，利用截断泊松核将座位容量分配到起飞前各小时，并通过时间融合变换器（Temporal Fusion Transformer）结合历史吞吐量、航班活动和时间变量进行预测。在亚特兰大机场的实验中，六小时直接预测的加权平均绝对百分比误差为9.33%，优于RNN（12.16%）和LSTM（11.37%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安检岗位排班需要准确预测安检需求的到来时间，但航班时刻表记录的是起飞时间而非旅客到达安检口的时间，两者之间存在时间错位，传统方法无法直接利用航班数据生成与安检吞吐量时间对齐的预测信号。

Method: 使用2023-2024年TSA吞吐量数据和Cirium Diio航班时刻表，将国内外座位容量通过截断泊松核分布到起飞前各小时，生成航班到达强度信号；随后使用时间融合变换器（Temporal Fusion Transformer）融合这些信号与历史吞吐量、航班计划和时间变量；按时间顺序训练模型，以2024年7-12月为测试集，与RNN和LSTM进行对比，每个模型使用五个随机种子评估。

Result: 六小时直接预测中，所提模型加权MAPE为9.33%，优于RNN的12.16%和LSTM的11.37%，且高峰期误差最低；六小时递归更新的24-96小时预测误差维持在10.60%-11.04%之间，但更长预测时域内有效预测起点较少。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将航班离港计划转化为可解释的起飞前安检负载信号，无需旅客-航班匹配即可支持提前排班、通道开放和多日安检规划。但由于观测吞吐量反映的是实际处理而非无约束到达量，预测结果需结合本地人员配置、容量、排队和等待时间信息共同解读。

Abstract: Checkpoint staffing requires accurate forecasts of when screening demand will occur, yet flight schedules record departure times rather than passenger arrival times at security checkpoints. This study develops a framework that converts known flight schedules into temporally aligned signals for forecasting hourly checkpoint throughput. Using 2023-2024 Transportation Security Administration throughput data and Cirium Diio flight schedules for Hartsfield-Jackson Atlanta International Airport, domestic and international seat capacity was distributed across pre-departure hours using truncated Poisson kernels. A Temporal Fusion Transformer then combined these schedule-derived arrival-intensity signals with historical throughput, scheduled activity, and temporal variables. Models were trained chronologically, with July-December 2024 reserved for testing, and evaluated against recurrent neural network and long short-term memory models across five random seeds. For direct six-hour forecasts, the proposed model achieved a weighted mean absolute percentage error of 9.33%, compared with 12.16% for the recurrent neural network and 11.37% for long short-term memory, while also producing the lowest errors during peak periods. With six-hour recursive updates, errors remained between 10.60% and 11.04% across 24-96 hour horizons, although longer horizons contained fewer valid forecast origins. By transforming scheduled departures into interpretable pre-departure screening-load signals without requiring passenger-flight matching, the framework supports advance staffing, lane-opening, and multiday checkpoint planning. Because observed throughput reflects realized processing rather than unconstrained arrivals, the forecasts should be interpreted together with local staffing, capacity, queue, and wait-time information.

</details>


### [282] [SP3O: Reinforcement Learning from Segment Preferences without Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02951)
*Evan Assmus,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SP3O，一种无需奖励模型、无需评论家且基于梯度的偏好强化学习算法，支持分段（segment）级别的偏好反馈，通过离线策略重要性采样构建策略价值差异估计器，并采用PPO型损失函数计算策略梯度，在机器人控制和LLM微调任务中展现出优于现有方法的性能，尤其在长时域任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好强化学习（PbRL）方法存在诸多不足：基于奖励模型的方法训练成本高；无奖励模型的方法要么局限于bandit或确定性MDP（如DPO、P3O），要么使用零阶无梯度优化导致收敛速度慢；且几乎所有无奖励模型方法都依赖轨迹级反馈，当轨迹较长时人类评估成本过高。分段（segment）更短更易比较，但现有方法缺乏对其的有效利用。

Method: SP3O采用分段级别的偏好反馈，通过离线策略重要性采样（off-policy importance sampling）构建精确的策略价值差异估计器，随后利用该估计器通过PPO类型的损失函数计算策略梯度。算法无需奖励模型和评论家网络，且是梯度优化的方法。论文还从理论上分析了分段长度选择的权衡。

Result: 在机器人控制和LLM微调实验中，SP3O相比其他PbRL/RLHF算法展现了改进的性能，特别是在长时域任务中优势明显。

Conclusion: SP3O是首个兼容分段偏好的、无奖励模型、无评论家且基于梯度的PbRL算法，既降低了人类评估负担（分段反馈），又避免了零阶优化的慢收敛问题，在理论和实验层面均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (PbRL) for general stochastic MDPs often requires training a reward model. Existing reward-model-free methods are either restricted to bandits or deterministic MDPs, such as DPO or P3O, or use zeroth-order, gradient-free optimization, which in general exhibits a slower convergence rate than gradient-based algorithms. Furthermore, existing reward-model-free preference-based RL algorithms almost exclusively use trajectory-level feedback, which can require significant effort from a human evaluator when trajectories are long. On the other hand, segments are much shorter, so they are easier to compare and evaluate. In this paper, we introduce a novel reward-model-free, critic-free, and gradient-based PbRL algorithm compatible with segment preferences named Segment Pairwise Proximal Policy Optimization (SP3O). SP3O utilizes segment-level preference feedback to construct an accurate policy value difference estimator via off-policy importance sampling, and then uses the estimator to compute the policy gradient via a PPO-type loss function. We provide a theoretical basis for the algorithm and analyze the tradeoff in choosing the segment length. We also evaluate it experimentally against other PbRL/RLHF algorithms in robotic control and LLM finetuning settings to show its improved performance, especially in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [283] [Inverted Detection and Control in Steering Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02957)
*Max Torop,Aria Masoomi,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种"反转转向向量"（ISVs）现象：某些判别性很强的转向向量虽然与概念的正向表征对齐，但施加转向时却会持续促进相反的行为。作者提供了ISV效应的几何特征描述，并提出了一种无需生成或响应评分即可区分ISV的方法，通过定向翻转符号改进了基于检测的转向流程（ITI），在27/30个实验中取得了+0.9%到+138%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转向向量（SVs）被广泛用于调控大语言模型输出中的概念表达，其核心假设是向量对概念具有线性判别性。然而作者发现存在一种"反转检测-控制"现象，即某些高判别性的转向向量会反向作用，这挑战了SVs的基本假设，理解并解决这一问题对于可靠地使用转向向量至关重要。

Method: 作者首先对ISV的效应进行几何表征分析，发现沿这些方向转向会在下游判别头中系统性地将表征推向"概念不存在"的状态（甚至在解码前就发生）。基于此分析，提出了一种无需生成文本或评分响应即可区分ISV的方法，从而能够针对性地翻转符号，并将其集成到基于检测的转向管道（Inference Time Intervention, ITI）中。

Result: 该方法在27/30个实验中取得了改进，提升幅度从+0.9%到+138%。实验覆盖了Gemma 3 12B、Qwen 2.5 14B和Olmo 3 7B三个模型以及5个不同概念。

Conclusion: ISV现象揭示了转向向量中检测与控制之间可能出现的分离，作者提出的几何分析方法和无生成的ISV识别策略能够有效改进现有转向管道的性能，为转向向量的可靠应用提供了新的理论理解和实用工具。

Abstract: Steering vectors (SVs) are widely used to influence the expression of concepts (e.g., truthfulness) in large language model outputs. A key assumption underpinning SVs is that they are linearly discriminative with respect to the concept: representations of texts that exhibit the concept are more aligned with the SV than those that do not, motivating shifts along the positive or negative SV direction to respectively promote or suppress the concept. In this work, we identify an inverted detection-control phenomenon in which some highly discriminative SVs that are aligned with positive representations can consistently promote the opposite behavior. We refer to such vectors as inverted-steering vectors (ISVs). We provide a geometric characterization of ISVs' effects, finding that steering along these directions systematically pushes representations in discriminative downstream heads as if the concept were absent, even prior to decoding. Motivated by this analysis, we propose an approach for distinguishing ISVs without requiring generation or associated response scoring. This enables targeted sign flips, which we use to improve a foundational detection-based steering pipeline via Inference Time Intervention (ITI). Our approach improves results in 27/30 experiments, ranging from +0.9% to +138%. We evaluate our findings on Gemma 3 12B, Qwen 2.5 14B, and Olmo 3 7B across 5 concepts.

</details>


### [284] [Scaling an Autoregressive Transformer for Single-Cell Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02961)
*Aleksandr Sharipov,Yusif Mukhtarov,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个单细胞基因表达向量的自监督生成任务：给定某细胞类型的一组基因表达向量，生成该细胞类型的额外基因表达向量。模型采用因果Transformer配合学习得到的量化VAE分词器，通过交叉熵损失训练。作者评估了生成向量的生物学保真度以及预训练损失的缩放行为，并首次为单细胞基础模型拟合了双指数缩放定律和计算最优前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基因表达数据对于理解细胞类型和生物学过程至关重要，但获取大量高质量数据成本高昂。本文旨在探索能否通过自监督生成模型，基于已有的细胞类型基因表达向量生成额外的合成数据，从而缓解数据稀缺问题，并系统研究该任务中模型规模与数据量的缩放规律。

Method: 采用因果Transformer与学习得到的量化VAE分词器相结合，使用交叉熵损失进行自监督训练。通过条件生成方式，给定hold-out的某细胞类型基因表达向量，生成该细胞类型的向量，并将生成分布与真实分布进行比较以评估保真度。通过变化参数量和训练数据量来研究缩放性质，拟合双指数缩放定律并计算计算最优前沿。

Result: 发现了首个单细胞基础模型的联合拟合双指数缩放定律和计算最优前沿。生成向量在生物学保真度上通过了验证，表明模型能够有效生成特定细胞类型的基因表达数据。

Conclusion: 该预训练模型展现出良好的缩放行为和生成能力，未来可将其微调用于扰动响应预测，为单细胞数据扩充和下游生物学任务提供支持。

Abstract: We study a self-supervised generation task for single-cell gene expression vectors: given a set of vectors from a cell type, we aim to generate additional gene expression vectors of that cell type. For this task we characterize both the biological fidelity of the generated gene expression vectors and the scaling behavior of the pretraining loss. The model is a causal transformer paired with a learned quantized VAE tokenizer, trained with a cross-entropy loss. To evaluate the model, we condition it on held-out gene expression vectors of a cell type and generate vectors of gene expression, comparing the resulting distribution over gene expression vectors to the ground truth distribution of that cell type. We study the scaling properties of the proposed architecture by varying the number of trained parameters and the amount of training data. To our knowledge, we find the first jointly-fit two-exponent scaling law and compute-optimal frontier for a single-cell foundation model. Finally, we discuss how this pretrained model could be finetuned for perturbation response prediction.

</details>


### [285] [A Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator for Transient Magnetization Prediction in Power Magnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02965)
*Yachao Zhu,Qiujie Huang,Sinan Li,Yang Li,Gang Lei,Jianguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息混合神经算子(PI-HNO)，一种紧凑的材料特异性神经网络模型，用于高频、高功率密度变换器中磁芯的瞬态磁化预测。该模型结合局部循环分支和Preisach启发的全局分支，并加入B-H能量一致性正则化，在MagNetX数据库的14种铁氧体材料上实现了1.92%的平均能量一致性误差，仅需4777个可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频高功率密度变换器中的磁芯元件面临非正弦磁通密度波形、快速转换、小磁滞回环、直流偏置和温度变化等复杂工况，传统的稳态磁芯损耗公式和单值材料曲线无法充分捕捉瞬态磁化响应，需要更精确的瞬态磁化预测模型。

Method: 提出PI-HNO模型：输入测量得到的B(t)-H(t)历史、预测区间的B(t)序列及工况信息，输出H(t)序列和重建的B-H轨迹。模型包含两个分支——局部循环分支用于边界状态表示和速率相关响应演化，Preisach启发的全局分支提取波形级磁滞上下文，并通过B-H能量一致性正则化约束模型物理一致性。

Result: 在MagNetX瞬态数据库上对14种铁氧体材料进行材料特异性建模，PI-HNO实现了序列精度与B(t)-H(t)能量一致性之间的紧凑权衡，平均和95分位B-H能量一致性误差分别为1.92%和7.60%，每个模型仅使用4777个可训练参数。消融实验表明局部、全局和能量感知正则化组件对瞬态磁化预测均有独特贡献。

Conclusion: PI-HNO以极少的参数量实现了高精度的瞬态磁化预测，验证了物理信息约束的混合神经算子方法在磁芯损耗建模中的有效性，局部与全局分支的设计以及能量一致性正则化对于提升模型性能均不可或缺。

Abstract: Magnetic components in high-frequency, high-power-density converters are increasingly driven by non-sinusoidal flux-density waveforms with fast transitions, minor-loop operation, dc bias, and temperature variation. Under these conditions, steady-state core-loss formulas and single-valued material curves cannot fully capture transient magnetization responses. This work proposes the Physics-Informed Hybrid Neural Operator (PI-HNO), a compact material-specific neural model with B-H energy-consistency regularization for core-loss-oriented transient magnetization prediction. Given the measured B(t)-H(t) history, the input B(t) series over the prediction interval and operating-condition information, PI-HNO predicts the H(t) series and the corresponding reconstructed B-H trajectory. The model integrates a local recurrent branch for boundary-state representation and rate-dependent response evolution with a Preisach-inspired global branch that extracts waveform-level hysteresis context. Evaluation on the MagNetX transient database using material-specific models for 14 ferrite materials demonstrates that PI-HNO achieves a compact trade-off between sequence accuracy and B(t)-H(t) energy consistency, with the mean and 95th percentile B(t)-H(t) energy consistency errors of 1.92% and 7.60%, respectively, using only 4777 trainable parameters per model. Ablation studies further demonstrate that the local, global, and energy-aware regularized components provide distinct contributions to transient magnetization prediction.

</details>


### [286] [Temporal Leakage in LLM Backtesting: Measurement, Validation, and Adjusted Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02985)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM回测中传统的污染检测方法（比较训练截止前后的分数）是无效的，因为模型对截止时间附近的知识天然更熟悉，使"近因效应"被误认为数据泄露。作者提出两种基于外部信息的测量方法：已知截止时间识别边界泄露，以及匹配的清洁对照组实现全局检测并给出泄露调整分数。在孪生模型中植入泄露验证了估计器的有效性，部署到前沿模型上发现一个截止局部特征，并澄清五个模型的表现优势仅是近因效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回测中的污染检测方法（对比截止前后分数）被证明无信息量——四个旗舰模型在不可能记忆的截止后问题上仍会"失败"该检测。作者从结构上解释这一现象：模型对接近截止时间的问题天然掌握更多信息，近因效应会伪装成泄露。因此需要更严谨的测量方法而非简单检测。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明任何被动回测（passive backtest）都无法区分真正的技能、近因效应和数据泄露。随后提出两种外部信息来源：（1）已知截止时间可用于识别边界处的泄露；（2）匹配的清洁对照组可实现全局泄露检测并输出泄露调整后的分数。还推导出泄露集中出现在"让大众意外且训练数据覆盖充分"的结果上，且部分记忆会获得不成比例的回报。通过孪生模型植入泄露验证估计器能恢复注入剂量并对清洁问题返回零结果。

Result: 在孪生模型实验中，估计器准确恢复了植入的泄露剂量，并在干净问题上返回零。部署到前沿模型时，检测到一个截止局部化的泄露特征；在审计的检测能力下限条件下，五个模型的表观优势被判定为仅由近因效应造成，而非真实泄露。

Conclusion: LLM回测不应被废弃，只需一个可靠的参考基准（如已知截止时间或匹配的清洁对照组）即可将其转化为有意义的测量工具，实现泄露检测与分数校正。

Abstract: The standard check for contamination in LLM backtests is simple: compare scores before and after the training cutoff. We show this check is uninformative. Four flagship models fail it on questions they cannot have memorized: every scored question resolved after their cutoffs. The reason is structural. Models legitimately know more about times near their cutoff, so recency mimics leakage, and we prove no passive backtest can separate the two from genuine skill. Measurement, not just detection, requires information from outside the backtest. We supply it in two forms. A known cutoff identifies leakage at the boundary; a matched clean control identifies it globally and yields a leakage-adjusted score. We also derive where leakage hides: it concentrates on outcomes that surprised the crowd and were well covered in training, and partial memorization is disproportionately rewarded. We validate the estimators against ground truth by planting leakage in twin models, where they recover the injected dose and return null on clean questions. Deployed on frontier models, they detect one cutoff-localized signature and, at the audit's power floor, clear five models whose apparent advantages were recency alone. Backtests need not be discarded; they need one defensible reference.

</details>


### [287] [AcceptMoE: Commitment-Weighted Self-Sizing Verifier Expert Sets for Efficient MoE Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02989)
*Shuang Liang,Hao,Chen,Zhiwen Mo,Qianzhou Wang,Guoyu Li,Lingxiao Ma,Wayne Luk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AcceptMoE是一种针对MoE目标模型的验证端专家选择器，通过结合路由器分数与离线估计的承诺概率，自动调整每个验证块的专家数量，并在卸载场景下基于缓存驻留状态而非预取来限制专家资格，在几乎不损失准确率的情况下显著提升吞吐量并减少数据传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MoE目标模型的推测解码中，并行验证一棵草稿token树会激活所有树节点选中的专家并集，但只有少数节点被采纳，导致token工作量、激活专家并集规模和专家权重传输成为不同的成本度量，仅减少token工作量不一定能缩小专家并集，且卸载场景下传输流量还取决于缓存驻留情况。现有方法缺乏自动调整专家数量的机制。

Method: AcceptMoE提出一种验证端专家选择方法：将目标路由器分数与离线估计的承诺概率相结合，自动调整每个验证块可用的专家数量，无需用户指定专家预算；在专家卸载场景下，根据缓存驻留状态而非预测自然路由来条件化专家资格，并预取相应专家权重。

Result: 在12个模型-任务对（覆盖3个MoE目标和4个基准）上，AcceptMoE的平均准确率比EAGLE-3推测解码（自然路由）仅低0.27个百分点；使用SGLang服务、批大小为1时，在全部专家权重驻留GPU内存情况下达到基线1.290倍吞吐量，在物理专家卸载情况下达到2.06倍吞吐量，同时将主机到设备的数据传输减少73.6%至77.1%。

Conclusion: 尽管约束目标专家资格会改变模型分布，AcceptMoE在准确率上几乎无损失，却能显著提升MoE推测解码的吞吐量（尤其在专家卸载场景下），并大幅降低数据传输开销，是一种有效的MoE推测解码优化方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding verifies a tree of draft tokens in one target-model forward pass. For a mixture-of-experts (MoE) target, however, parallel verification can activate the union of the experts selected by all tree nodes, even though only a small subset of those nodes reaches the accepted output. Token count, activated-expert union size, and expert-weight traffic are therefore distinct cost measures: reducing the token workload need not shrink the expert union proportionally, and under offloading, transfer traffic also depends on cache residency. We introduce AcceptMoE, a verifier-side expert selector that combines target-router scores with offline-estimated commitment probabilities and automatically adjusts the number of eligible experts for each verification block, eliminating the need for a user-specified expert budget. Under offloading, AcceptMoE conditions expert eligibility on cache residency instead of predicting natural routes and prefetching the corresponding expert weights. Although constraining target-expert eligibility changes the model distribution, across 12 model-task pairs spanning three MoE targets and four benchmarks, AcceptMoE's mean accuracy is 0.27 percentage points lower than that of EAGLE-3 speculative decoding with natural routing. Served with SGLang at batch size one, it reaches 1.290 times the throughput of this baseline with all expert weights in GPU memory, and 2.06 times under physical expert offloading, while reducing host-to-device traffic by 73.6 percent to 77.1 percent.

</details>


### [288] [Joint Affine Spectral Shaping: Coupling Weight and Bias Updates Beyond Weight-Only Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02991)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究矩阵谱优化器中权重更新谱与偏置更新的分离效应，提出联合规则化逆谱（JRI）方法，将仿射层权重和偏置作为联合动量矩阵进行整体谱映射。在BERT-mini IMDb任务上，JRI将测试准确率提升至85.738±0.180%，优于权重独立谱优化（85.562%），并通过22次实验复现验证了结果的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵谱优化器通常仅对权重矩阵应用谱变换，而将向量偏置委托给独立的优化器（如Adam）。作者旨在探究这种分离是否中性，即联合处理权重和偏置的谱分配是否能带来额外收益。

Method: 将每个仿射层构建为联合动量矩阵A=[M_W, αm_b]，对完整矩阵应用带帽正则化逆谱映射，同时产生权重和物理偏置更新。通过严格五种子消融实验比较精确SVD Muon、权重独立逆整形、仿射探针逆整形和联合规则化逆谱（JRI）四种方法。

Result: JRI在验证损失选择的测试准确率上达到85.738±0.180%，测试损失降至0.3291，五个种子均优于基线。在性能峰值窗口，JRI保持权重更新范数的同时将偏置更新范数从0.02095降至0.00301，边界函数份额从86.58%降至78.97%，偏置与权重诱导边界运动的余弦从+0.030变为-0.137。22种子独立复现获得85.743±0.203%的准确率。

Conclusion: 联合仿射谱分配是权重独立谱优化的一致但小幅的扩展，表明将偏置纳入联合谱变换能带来稳定的性能提升，但增益幅度有限。

Abstract: Matrix spectral optimizers reshape weight-update spectra but usually delegate vector-valued biases to a separate optimizer. We study whether this separation is neutral. We formulate each affine layer as a joint momentum matrix $A=[M_W,αm_b]$ and apply a capped regularized-inverse spectral map to the complete matrix, producing both the weight and physical bias updates. A strict five-seed ablation on a four-layer BERT-mini trained from scratch on IMDb compares exact-SVD Muon, weight-only inverse shaping, affine-probe inverse shaping, and the proposed joint regularized inverse (JRI). Weight-only inverse shaping raises validation-loss-selected test accuracy from $84.903\pm0.242\%$ to $85.562\pm0.308\%$ and lowers selected test loss from $0.3479$ to $0.3345$. Allowing bias to alter the joint SVD while retaining an independent Adam bias update does not improve over weight-only inverse shaping. Using the transformed bias jointly raises selected test accuracy to $85.738\pm0.180\%$ and lowers test loss to $0.3291$, with all five seeds improving relative to the probe baseline. During the peak-performance window, JRI preserves the eligible weight-update norm while reducing the bias-update norm from $0.02095$ to $0.00301$, lowers boundary-function share from $86.58\%$ to $78.97\%$, and changes the cosine between weight-induced boundary motion and explicit bias from $+0.030$ to $-0.137$. An independent 22-seed replication yields $85.743\pm0.203\%$ selected test accuracy. These results identify joint affine spectral allocation as a small but consistent extension to weight-only spectral optimization.

</details>


### [289] [A Graph Signal Processing Perspective on Numerical Sequence Representations in LLM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03015)
*Jiajun Bao,Zihao Qi,Toni J. B. Liu,Gurbir Arora,Raphaël Sarfati,Nicolas Boullé,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从图信号处理视角研究大语言模型在进行上下文学习（ICL）数值推理时，其内部表示如何组织数值信息。研究发现，随着上下文长度增加，表示会依据输入动力学复杂度产生系统性分化：简单输入产生更强的全局连通性和更平滑的频谱集中信号，复杂输入则产生更局部化的图和更宽的频谱支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要通过输出级评估（如预测误差）来刻画LLM的数值推理能力，但对数值信息在LLM内部表示中如何组织缺乏理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究注意力诱导的token图结构与隐藏状态信号之间的内在关联。

Method: 采用图信号处理视角，将注意力视为token上的加权图、token隐藏状态视为节点上的信号。通过定量图谱诊断（graph-spectral diagnostics）和定性token图可视化两种方式，分析不同输入动力学复杂度下表示的差异，并考察上下文长度变化的影响。

Result: 简单输入产生注意力诱导token图的更强全局连通性和更平滑、频谱集中的隐藏状态信号；复杂输入则产生更局部化的图、更宽的频谱支撑和更高频能量。这些内部签名在不同模型家族中保持一致。

Conclusion: 数值ICL过程中存在系统性、上下文依赖的内部签名，这些签名在跨模型家族中具有保守性，揭示了LLM内部数值表示组织的新规律。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated in-context learning (ICL) capabilities for numerical inference over sequences serialized as text. Prior work has identified and characterized this form of numerical inference primarily through output-level evaluations such as prediction error. However, how numerical information is organized within LLM representations remains much less understood. To study this internal organization, we adopt a graph signal processing perspective in which attention induces a weighted graph over tokens, while token hidden states define signals on its nodes. Quantitative graph-spectral diagnostics and qualitative token-graph visualizations reveal that representations become more clearly differentiated by input dynamical complexity as context length increases. Simpler inputs produce attention-induced token graphs with stronger global connectivity and smoother, spectrally concentrated hidden-state signals, whereas more complex inputs produce more localized graphs and hidden-state signals with broader spectral support and greater high-frequency energy. Together, these findings point to systematic, context-dependent internal signatures associated with numerical ICL that are conserved across model families.

</details>


### [290] [Paired Recipient-based Evaluation of Survival Prediction for Deceased Donor Kidney Transplants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03017)
*Misaki Matsuura,Mohammadreza Nemati,Dulat Bekbolsynov,Stanislaw Stepkowski,Kevin S. Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用SRTR数据中的死亡供体肾移植数据，提出了一个新颖的配对受体评估框架，通过比较同一供体分配的两个受体的移植结果来评估改变受体的反事实收益。五种不同复杂度的生存预测模型均达到约60%的配对受体准确率，并将该准确率转化为可解释的移植后年数获益。同时指出C-index在此场景下的局限性，证明提出的指标更具临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用机器学习预测肾移植结果（如移植物失效年限），潜在应用于移植前供体-受体匹配以改善术后结果。但现有评估指标（如C-index）在此场景下不够反映实际临床应用情况，需要更贴近真实分配环境的评估框架。

Method: 基于死亡供体肾移植数据（来自SRTR），训练五种不同复杂度的生存预测模型（从线性到深度学习）。提出配对受体评估框架：比较来自同一死亡供体的两个受体之间的移植物结果，评估改变受体的反事实收益，并转化为可解释的移植后年数获益。

Result: 五种生存预测模型（复杂度从线性到深度学习）均达到约60%的配对受体准确率；通过该指标可量化移植后获得的年数；同时揭示了C-index在此场景下的局限性。

Conclusion: 提出的配对受体准确率指标比C-index更符合临床相关性和真实世界分配环境，为肾移植结果预测模型的评估提供了更有意义的新框架。

Abstract: There has been significant interest in using machine learning algorithms to predict kidney transplant outcomes, such as the number of years until a graft inevitably fails. These prediction algorithms could possibly be used for pre-transplant donor-recipient matching to identify more compatible donors and recipients and thus improve post-transplant outcomes. In this study, we explore the use of survival prediction models trained on deceased donor kidney transplant data from the Scientific Registry of Transplant Recipients (SRTR). We propose a novel paired recipient-based evaluation framework that compares graft outcomes between two recipients who received kidneys from the same deceased donor, allowing us to evaluate the counterfactual benefit of changing the recipient for a certain donor. We find that five different survival prediction models, ranging in complexity from linear to deep learning-based models, all result in ~60% paired recipient-based accuracy. We further translate this accuracy into an interpretable quantity of post-transplant years gained. We also highlight major limitations of the commonly used concordance index (C-index) metric for evaluating survival prediction accuracy in this setting and demonstrate that our proposed paired recipient-based accuracy metric is more clinically relevant and better reflects real-world allocation settings.

</details>


### [291] [PLAN: Parallel Liquid-Inspired Approximation Network for Efficient Representation Learning in Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03041)
*Dhivya Dharshini Kannan,Wei Zhang,Jieyi Bi,Yingpeng Du,Tianjun Wei,Jie Zhang,Zuming Liu,Anupam Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PLAN（并行液体启发近似网络），一种轻量级表示学习框架，用于柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP）。PLAN将液体神经网络（LNN）的连续液体状态动态重新表述为离散化、可并行的形式，在保持参数效率的同时解决了LNN顺序动态的计算瓶颈。PLAN将状态演化与上下文聚合解耦，并可作为即插即用的骨干网络推广到确定性、随机和多面动态FJSP变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度强化学习的FJSP方法依赖注意力中心架构，虽然性能优异但存在参数过多和推理延迟过高的问题。液体神经网络提供了参数高效的替代方案，但其固有的顺序动态特性成为计算效率的瓶颈，因此需要在表示学习效率和计算效率之间取得平衡。

Method: PLAN将连续液体状态动态重新表述为离散且可并行的形式，在结构上将状态演化与上下文聚合解耦：液体启发的更新负责主要的状态表示演化，轻量级上下文聚合模块提供补充的全局上下文。PLAN作为即插即用的骨干网络，可与紧凑随机模块配对处理随机FJSP，并可替代多面动态FJSP中的重异构图变换器。

Result: 在确定性、随机和多面动态FJSP基准测试中，PLAN相比最先进的基线分别将平均makespan降低了1.2%、1.4%和2.3%，在某个基准设置中改进幅度达10.2%。平均推理延迟分别降低了13.2%、31.7%和26.9%，最大降低69.2%，且仅使用基线参数的22%-47%。

Conclusion: PLAN成功解决了注意力架构参数过多与LNN顺序计算瓶颈之间的权衡问题，在多个FJSP变体上实现了更好的性能、更低的推理延迟和更少的参数，证明了液体启发的并行化表述在高计算效率与强表示能力之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches for flexible job shop scheduling (FJSP) heavily rely on attention-centric architectures to achieve state-of-the-art performance. However, these models suffer from excessive parameter counts and prohibitive inference latency as problem scales expand. While liquid neural networks (LNNs) offer a parameter-efficient alternative for modeling adaptive state evolution, their inherently sequential dynamics bottleneck computational efficiency. To resolve this trade-off, we propose PLAN (Parallel Liquid-inspired Approximation Network), a lightweight representation learning framework that reformulates continuous liquid-state dynamics into a discretized and parallelizable formulation. PLAN structurally decouples state evolution from context aggregation, where liquid-inspired updates handle the primary evolving state representation, and a lightweight context aggregation module provides complementary global context. Furthermore, PLAN acts as a versatile, plug-and-play backbone that generalizes to complex FJSP variants, pairing with a compact stochastic module for stochastic FJSP and replacing heavy heterogeneous graph transformers in multi-faceted dynamic FJSP. Extensive evaluations across deterministic, stochastic, and multi-faceted dynamic FJSP benchmarks show that PLAN reduces the average makespan by 1.2%, 1.4%, and 2.3%, respectively, compared with the corresponding state-of-the-art baselines, with the improvement reaching 10.2% in one benchmark setting. PLAN also reduces average inference latency by 13.2%, 31.7%, and 26.9%, respectively, with a maximum reduction of 69.2% on the largest instances, while using only 22$-$47% of the baseline parameters.

</details>


### [292] [Exploiting Separability in Multi-Scale Grey-Box Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03045)
*Joshua E. Hammond,Tyler A. Soderstrom,Brian A. Korgel,Michael Baldea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双层贝叶斯优化方法，用于处理灰盒优化问题——即决策变量分为黑盒变量（昂贵黑盒函数）和白盒变量（闭合形式方程）。外层用BO优化黑盒变量，内层用全局优化求解白盒子问题，GPR代理仅定义在黑盒变量空间，白盒约束由内层优化精确满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灰盒优化问题中黑盒变量和白盒变量天然分离，现有方法通常需要惩罚函数、机会约束或矩近似来处理约束，这些方法不够精确且效率低下。作者希望利用这种可分离性来避免上述近似手段。

Method: 提出双层重构方法：外层采用贝叶斯优化（BO）在仅包含黑盒变量的低维空间中优化标量目标函数；内层通过全局优化求解白盒子问题。高斯过程代理仅定义在黑盒变量上而非全部变量空间，白盒约束在内层优化器收敛到可行点时被精确满足，无需惩罚函数、机会约束或矩近似。

Result: 在13个基准问题上，双层贝叶斯优化（bilevel BO）实现了更低的遗憾值（regret），且需要的迭代次数更少、墙钟时间更短。该方法对初始集大小、探索参数和内层求解器的选择均具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双层BO方法通过利用灰盒问题的结构化可分离性，在效率和精度上均优于传统方法，且不依赖近似约束处理技术，具有广泛的适用性和稳健性。

Abstract: We consider grey-box optimization problems where the decision variables naturally partition into black-box variables (as arguments to an expensive black-box function) and white-box variables, governed by a set of explicit, closed-form equations that also depend on the output of the black-box function. We exploit this separability through a bilevel reformulation: an outer Bayesian optimization (BO) to optimize the scalar objective as a function of black-box variables alone, while an inner problem solves the white-box subproblem via global optimization. The Gaussian process surrogate used in BO is therefore defined rather than and white-box constraints are satisfied exactly whenever the inner optimizer converges to a feasible point---without penalty functions, chance constraints, or moment approximations. On a suite of 13 benchmark problems, bilevel BO achieves lower regret, with fewer iterations and wall clock time. This advantage is robust to initialization set size, exploration parameters, and inner-solver choice.

</details>


### [293] [Revisiting TD Target Aggregation under Uncertainty in Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03069)
*Lipeng Zu,Xiaonan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SADQ算法，通过引入一步rollout预测来约束Q-learning中TD目标的聚合过程，从而缓解引导式时间差更新中的过估计问题，在多个基准任务上提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQN通过贪心最大化操作聚合下一状态的动作价值，该方法对估计噪声高度敏感：当Q值不确定时，最大化算子会确定性偏向最大估计值而忽略其可靠性，导致误差通过bootstrapping被放大。

Method: SADQ使用学习到的动力学模型生成一步rollout预测，来指导候选下一状态动作之间的比较，在TD目标形成过程中引入额外结构。该混合Bellman更新能够衰减不可靠的最大值，同时随着模型误差趋于零仍保持标准不动点。

Result: 理论分析表明SADQ能逐点降低bootstrap引起的过估计。实验上，在经典控制任务、真实世界向量环境和Atari基准测试中，SADQ相比强DQN变体一致提升了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: SADQ提供了一种简单有效的DQN修改方案，通过对TD目标聚合步骤的轻量级正则化，在保持原有学习框架的前提下有效缓解Q-learning中的过估计问题。

Abstract: Deep Q-Networks (DQNs) learn value functions through bootstrapped temporal-difference updates, where future returns are approximated using a greedy maximization over next-state action values. While effective, this aggregation rule is inherently sensitive to estimation noise: when Q-values are uncertain, the maximization operator deterministically favors the largest estimate, regardless of its reliability, leading to amplified errors through bootstrapping. In this work, we propose the \textbf{S}uccessor Rollout \textbf{A}ggregation \textbf{D}eep \textbf{Q}-Network (SADQ), a simple modification to Q-learning that regularizes how the TD target is formed. SADQ uses one-step rollout predictions from a learned dynamics model to guide the comparison among candidate next-state actions, introducing additional structure into the aggregation step without altering the underlying learning framework. The resulting mixed Bellman update attenuates unreliable maxima while preserving the standard fixed point under diminishing model error. We provide theoretical analysis showing that SADQ reduces bootstrap-induced overestimation in a pointwise manner. Empirically, SADQ consistently improves training stability across classical control tasks, real-world vector-based environments, and Atari benchmarks when compared to strong DQN variants.

</details>


### [294] [SynEnergy: Anomaly Semantic-Guided Diffusion for Synthetic Energy Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03087)
*Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ravikumar Gelli,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynEnergy是一个两阶段扩散框架，用于生成保留异常事件的细粒度能源消耗数据。它通过异构图异常语义学习（HG-ASL）提取区域特定的异常语义，再通过异常语义引导扩散（AS-Diff）将异常模式注入去噪过程。在四个真实数据集上，相比11个基线模型，异常保留保真度平均提升12.21%，下游质量提升2.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度能源消耗数据对需求预测、需求响应规划和电网可靠性评估至关重要，但隐私问题限制数据共享。现有合成数据生成方法虽能复现整体分布和周期模式，却常平滑掉或低估由极端天气、基础设施故障和行为变化引起的异常事件。这些异常事件稀疏、时间和空间局部化，且受地理邻近性和区域属性的异质依赖关系影响，难以保留。

Method: 提出SynEnergy两阶段扩散框架。第一阶段HG-ASL（异构图异常语义学习）通过联合建模城市区域间的空间与属性依赖，从稀疏残差结构中提取区域特定的异常语义。第二阶段AS-Diff（异常语义引导扩散）将学习到的异常语义注入去噪过程，生成保留异常模式且真实感强的消耗序列。该设计支持单区域可控生成并可扩展到城市级规模。

Result: 在四个真实世界能源消耗数据集上与11个通用及能源专用生成基线对比，SynEnergy在异常保留保真度上平均提升12.21%，下游任务质量提升2.96%，同时整体生成保真度与基线持平。

Conclusion: SynEnergy有效解决了合成能源数据生成中异常事件难以保留的挑战，证明了通过显式建模异常语义并注入扩散生成过程，可以在不牺牲整体生成质量的前提下显著提升异常保留能力。

Abstract: Fine-grained energy consumption data are essential for applications such as demand forecasting, demand response planning, and grid reliability assessment. However, access to such data is often restricted by privacy concerns and data-sharing constraints, motivating growing interest in synthetic energy data generation. Although existing methods can reproduce overall consumption distributions and recurring temporal patterns, they often smooth out or underrepresent anomalous events caused by extreme weather, infrastructure failures, and behavioral shifts. Preserving these events is challenging because they are sparse, localized in time and space, and shaped by heterogeneous dependencies across geographical proximity and regional attributes. To address these challenges, we propose SynEnergy, a two-stage diffusion-based framework for anomaly-preserving energy consumption data generation. The first stage, Heterogeneous Graph-based Anomaly Semantic Learning (HG-ASL), extracts region-specific anomaly semantics from sparse residual structures by jointly modeling spatial and attribute dependencies across urban regions. The second stage, Anomaly Semantic-guided Diffusion (AS-Diff), injects the learned anomaly semantics into the denoising process to generate realistic consumption sequences while preserving anomalous patterns. This design enables controllable generation for individual regions and scales naturally to city-wide settings. We evaluate SynEnergy on four real-world energy consumption datasets against 11 general-purpose and energy-specific generation baselines. Experimental results show that SynEnergy improves anomaly preservation fidelity by an average of 12.21% and downstream quality by 2.96%, while maintaining competitive overall generation fidelity compared to baselines.

</details>


### [295] [SMOPD: Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03092)
*Wen Wang,Jiahua Bao,Tu Yongsiqi,Yihao Liu,Haotian Zhou,Haoxuan Ma,Mengyu Zhou,Wenkui Fan,Junwei He,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMOPD（Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation），一种两阶段多奖励强化学习训练方法。针对现有GDPO方法在稠密奖励（0.1-1.0细粒度分数）与稀疏奖励（0/1二值反馈）混合时稀疏奖励优化信号不足的问题，SMOPD通过"专业化-合并"两阶段训练：先训练多个奖励专业化的教师模型，再通过在线策略蒸馏将教师能力合并到单个学生策略中。实验证明在1.5B、3B和7B规模下均优于GDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GDPO方法虽然通过逐奖励维度归一化缓解了奖励信号相互掩盖的问题，但在奖励粒度差异较大的场景（如稠密奖励与稀疏奖励混合）下仍无法有效平衡。稀疏奖励因优化信号不足导致其对应能力无法被有效强化，因此需要一种方法既能增强稀疏奖励的优化信号，又不牺牲已从细粒度奖励中学到的能力。

Method: SMOPD采用两阶段训练范式：Stage1-Specialize阶段，使用奖励优先级配置训练多个奖励专用的教师模型，确保每个奖励在其信号能有效驱动优化的条件下被充分学习；Stage2-Merge阶段，利用在线策略蒸馏技术将各奖励专用教师的能力融合到单一学生策略中，同时保持任务级优化的平衡。

Result: 在互补奖励设置（工具调用准确性和格式）和冲突奖励设置（有用性和无害性）两类多奖励场景下，SMOPD在1.5B、3B和7B三种模型规模上均优于GDPO基线方法。

Conclusion: SMOPD通过将多奖励优化分解为"各奖励专业化学习"和"能力在线蒸馏合并"两个阶段，有效解决了不同粒度奖励信号间的平衡问题，为多奖励强化学习提供了一种可行且可扩展的训练策略。

Abstract: We aim to improve model performance in multi-reward reinforcement learning training process. Existing Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization (GDPO) has mitigated the issue of reward signals masking one another during direct scalarization by normalizing each reward dimension separately before aggregation. However, our experiments show that GDPO still struggles to balance reward signals with different granularities. Specifically, in some particular training tasks, the model may receive a dense reward that assigns fine-grained scores ranging from 0.1 to 1.0, together with a sparse reward that provides only binary feedback of either 0 or 1. In such cases, we find that the sparse reward may provide an insufficient optimization signal, preventing its corresponding capability from being effectively reinforced. Therefore, how can we strengthen the optimization signal from the sparse reward without sacrificing the capability already learned from the fine-grained reward? To overcome this limitation, we propose Specialize-and-Merge Online Policy Distillation (SMOPD), a two-stage training method for multi-reward optimization. Stage1-Specialize: SMOPD first employs reward-priority configurations to train multiple reward-specialized teachers, allowing each reward to be learned under conditions where its signal can effectively drive optimization. Stage2-Merge: SMOPD then utilizes online policy distillation to combine the reward-specialized capabilities of these teachers into a single student policy, while maintaining balanced task-level optimization. To validate our method, we conduct experiments on two multi-reward settings: complementary rewards(tool-calling accuracy and format) and conflicting rewards (helpful and harmless rewards). Based on above settings, SMOPD outperforms GDPO across 1.5B, 3B and 7B backbones.

</details>


### [296] [Convex-Hull-Neighborhood Smooth Dual Generalization: Controlling Local Correction Propagation in Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03108)
*Yi Yang,Zhennan Chen,Mingfeng Lv,Hanlei Li,Zhengsen Ruan,Lvqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出凸包邻域平滑对偶泛化（CSDG）方法，将离线强化学习的Bellman备份表达为样本内值目标加上CHN局部修正项，通过不同扰动半径下采样并平滑样本内与OOD候选来构造修正项，并以混合系数控制其对备份的贡献。理论上推导了精确的一步修正恒等式、时变迭代界和不动点界，实践上用非对称有界噪声和expectile回归近似实现，在Gym-MuJoCo和AntMaze上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中，临近分布外（OOD）动作虽可带来收益，但自举（bootstrapping）会放大这些动作处的估计误差。现有正则化与局部泛化方法通常通过分离机制分别控制OOD区域或泛化目标的影响，缺乏统一且显式处理泛化贡献的框架。

Method: CSDG将Bellman备份分解为样本内值目标与CHN局部修正项之和。修正项通过在不同扰动半径下采样样本内导向和OOD导向的候选并进行平滑来获得，混合系数λ控制其贡献，递归折扣仍为γ。理论部分推导了一步修正恒等式、时变迭代界及依赖固定点处分支配度的不动点界；算法部分使用非对称有界噪声和expectile回归近似实现，无需精确支持分类或额外的悲观OOD惩罚。

Result: 在Gym-MuJoCo和AntMaze基准上展示了强聚合性能和稳定的值估计；理论分析给出了精确的一步修正恒等式、时变迭代界和只依赖固定点处分支配度的不动点界。

Conclusion: CSDG通过将Bellman备份分解为样本内参考路径与显式的CHN局部修正，统一且可解释地处理了OOD泛化问题，兼具理论保证与实用性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (offline RL) can benefit from nearby out-of-distribution (OOD) actions, but estimation errors at these actions may be amplified by bootstrapping. Existing regularization and local-generalization methods control either the admissible OOD region or the influence of generalized targets, often through separate mechanisms. We propose Convex Hull Neighborhood Smooth Dual Generalization (CSDG), which expresses the Bellman backup as an in-sample value target plus a CHN-local correction. This formulation makes the generalized contribution explicit and separates it from the in-sample reference path. The correction is obtained by smoothing in-sample-oriented and OOD-oriented candidates sampled at different perturbation radii. A mixture coefficient lambda scales its contribution to each backup, while the recursive discount remains gamma. Under boundedness and fixed perturbation kernels, we derive an exact one-step correction identity, a time-varying iterate bound, and a fixed-point bound that depends only on the branch discrepancy at the fixed point. We further characterize the implicit policies induced by the idealized operators and give a conditional non-degradation criterion. The practical algorithm approximates these quantities using asymmetric bounded noise and expectile regression, without exact support classification or an additional pessimistic OOD penalty. Experiments on Gym-MuJoCo and AntMaze show strong aggregate performance and stable value estimation. Code is available at: https://github.com/YOUNG-fnxm/CSDG

</details>


### [297] [Double Descent in Gradient Boosting Decision Trees via Split-Candidate Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03111)
*Ryuichi Kanoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"分裂候选数"（split candidates）作为梯度提升决策树（GBDT）的容量参数，通过实证树核诊断分析发现其数量增加会引发双重下降（double descent）现象，并在XGBoost、LightGBM和CatBoost中验证了这一规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双重下降现象通常通过显式容量参数（如神经网络宽度）进行研究，但GBDT一直缺乏类似的单轴容量参数，阻碍了对GBDT双重下降行为的系统分析。

Method: 提出分裂候选数作为GBDT的操作性容量参数；构建实证树核诊断工具，通过核秩增长和微小正特征值出现来识别噪声敏感方向；在多个GBDT框架（XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost）及随机森林对照上进行系统实验。

Result: 实验表明在中间分裂候选预算处测试误差出现峰值，支持双重下降现象；更深树在更少分裂候选下进入该区域，更大训练集需要更细网格，标签噪声会增强峰值；随机森林对照在同一扫描下单调改善。

Conclusion: 分裂候选数缩放可作为研究GBDT的单轴容量干预手段，观察到的双重下降源于候选诱导的几何结构与提升动力学之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Double descent is commonly studied by scaling an explicit capacity parameter, such as neural-network width. For gradient boosting decision trees (GBDTs), however, an analogous single-axis capacity parameter has not been established. We propose the number of split candidates as an operational capacity parameter for GBDTs. Holding other training controls fixed, increasing the split-candidate budget refines the feature-quantization grid and expands the dictionary of root-to-leaf paths from which boosting selects its updates. To analyze this expansion, we construct an empirical tree-kernel diagnostic that summarizes how candidate-induced paths group the training examples. A regime in which the empirical kernel rank grows toward the sample size and very small positive eigenvalues emerge exposes noise-sensitive directions; in this regime, test error peaks before decreasing again at larger split-candidate budgets. This perspective predicts that deeper trees should reach the regime with fewer split candidates, larger training sets should require finer grids, and label noise should make the peak more pronounced. Experiments support these predictions and show test-error peaks at intermediate split-candidate budgets across XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost, whereas a random-forest control improves monotonically under the same split-candidate sweep. Taken together, our analysis and experiments support split-candidate scaling as a single-axis capacity intervention for studying GBDTs and suggest that the observed double descent arises from an interaction between candidate-induced geometry and boosting dynamics.

</details>


### [298] [Simulation-free and finite-time diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03117)
*Kentaro Kaba,Masayuki Ohzeki,Yuki Sughiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种设计扩散模型参考过程的通用框架，能够同时实现免模拟训练和有限时间生成，并揭示了分数匹配并非扩散模型训练的本质，而是通过参考过程反转自然涌现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型需要在"免模拟训练"和"有限时间生成"之间进行权衡，作者希望找到一种能够同时兼顾两者的参考过程设计方法。

Method: 核心思想是先指定具有易处理形式的时间相关条件分布，然后构造一个以这些分布为边际分布的参考过程。该框架统一了扩散模型的训练范式，并推导出条件流匹配是该框架在小噪声极限下的特例。

Result: 证明了分数匹配不是扩散模型训练的必要条件，而是参考过程反转后自然产生的结果；条件流匹配是该框架的小噪声极限形式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种同时实现免模拟训练和有限时间生成的参考过程设计方案，为扩散模型的统一理解和改进提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The performance of generative diffusion models is determined by the choice of the reference diffusion process connecting the empirical and prior distributions. Conventional approaches typically trade off simulation-free training against finite-time generation. We propose a framework for designing the reference process that achieves both simultaneously. The key idea is to prescribe tractable time-dependent conditional distributions and then construct the reference process realizing them as its marginals. This framework reveals that score matching is not fundamental to diffusion-model training but instead emerges naturally through reversal of the reference process. We further show that conditional flow matching arises as the small-noise limit of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [299] [Trajectory-Guided Forget-Recover Network for Continual LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03123)
*Zezheng Wu,Xinghe Cheng,Qinggang Zhang,Haoran Luo,Jiapu Wang,Qing Yang,Jingwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TFR-Net框架，用于解决连续机器遗忘场景中遗忘知识重现和模型容量退化两大问题，通过追踪通道级风险和激活休眠通道来平衡遗忘效果与保留效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中机器遗忘请求是持续到达的，这带来两个挑战：一是遗忘干预可能导致目标相关知识重新分布到其他通道，使已遗忘知识重新出现；二是重复遗忘会逐渐减少模型容量，损害保留数据的效用。

Method: TFR-Net通过追踪跨请求的通道级风险，区分持续性目标相关通道与瞬时热点通道，仅抑制持续性通道；同时通过重新激活休眠通道来恢复模型容量，这些通道对保留效用有强贡献且当前和历史遗忘风险均较低，且仅在保留效用退化在预设容忍范围内时才接受恢复。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，TFR-Net在遗忘有效性与保留效用之间的权衡上持续优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: TFR-Net有效解决了连续机器遗忘中知识重现和容量退化的问题，实现了更优的遗忘效果与保留效用平衡。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to eliminate the influence of sensitive data on a model. In the real world, unlearning requests arrive continually, which gives rise to two challenges. First, an unlearning intervention may redistribute target-related computation across remaining pathways, allowing previously forgotten knowledge to re-emerge. Second, repeated unlearning interventions may progressively reduce the model capacity needed to preserve retained utility. To address these challenges, we propose the Trajectory-guided Forget-Recover Network (TFR-Net). TFR-Net tracks channel-level risk across requests. It separates persistent target-related channels from transient hotspots and suppresses only the persistent ones. TFR-Net also recovers model capacity by reactivating dormant channels. These channels make strong contributions to retained utility and show low current and historical forget risk. The recovery is accepted only when retained-utility degradation remains within a predefined tolerance. Experiments on four datasets show that TFR-Net consistently achieves a more favorable trade-off between unlearning effectiveness and retained utility than representative baselines.

</details>


### [300] [Lightweight Chunk Selection for Mobile Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03148)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Wen Yu,Jiefu Chen,Xin Fu,Renjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的RAG块选择方法，通过结合问题隐状态、MoE路由专家信号和检索块嵌入三类互补特征，用紧凑的MLP映射到证据原型空间，以余弦相似度选择最具有证据支撑性的检索块，在移动设备上相比基线平均提升2.5%的rank-1证据选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在移动和边缘设备上部署面临计算和内存开销大的问题，虽然可以通过只保留一个检索块来降低开销，但排名第一的检索块并不总是最具有证据支撑性的，因为检索相似性并不等同于证据充分性。现有的上下文缩减方法往往需要额外的LLM或压缩器，在严格的移动端预算下成本过高，因此需要一种轻量级的块选择方法。

Method: 提出将轻量级RAG块选择建模为证据对齐问题。选择器融合三类互补特征：1）问题隐状态（LLM侧查询意图）；2）MoE路由导出的专家信号（生成器内部路由结构）；3）检索块嵌入（候选侧证据几何）。通过紧凑的多层感知机将这些特征映射到块嵌入空间中的证据原型，用余弦相似度选择与该原型最对齐的候选块。同时引入可选的任务感知特征选择策略以进一步降低选择器输入维度，并基于证据充分性而非答案字符串包含构建语义块正确性标签用于监督评估。

Result: 在移动端适用的基线比较中，所提出的选择器在rank-1证据选择上平均提升了2.5%。实验结果验证了使用LLM侧查询表示和MoE路由信息并与检索侧候选嵌入对齐是移动端RAG块选择的有效且参数高效策略。

Conclusion: 利用LLM侧查询表示、MoE路由信息并与检索侧候选嵌入进行对齐，是一种在移动端RAG场景下既有效又参数高效的块选择策略，为移动端RAG的轻量化部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: RAG improves the factual grounding of LLM by incorporating external knowledge, but deploying RAG on mobile and edge devices remains challenging because retrieved context increases computation and memory. A direct way to reduce this cost is to retain only one retrieved chunk before generation, but the top-ranked retrieved chunk is not always the most evidence-supporting one, since retrieval similarity does not necessarily imply evidential sufficiency. Existing context-reduction methods can improve context quality, but often require additional LLMs or compressors that are costly under a strict mobile budget. In this paper, we study lightweight RAG chunk selection as an evidence-alignment problem. Our selector combines three complementary feature sources: question hidden states that represent LLM-side query intent, MoE routing-derived expert signals that capture the generator's internal routing structure, and retrieved chunk embeddings that preserve candidate-side evidence geometry. A compact multilayer perceptron maps these features to an evidence prototype in the chunk embedding space, and the candidate most aligned with this prototype is selected by cosine similarity. For stricter deployment budgets, we further introduce an optional task-aware feature selection strategy to reduce the selector input dimension. To support supervised evaluation, we construct semantic chunk-correctness labels based on evidence sufficiency rather than answer-string containment. Experiments show that the proposed selector consistently improves rank-1 evidence selection over mobile-applicable baselines by an average of 2.5%. These results suggest that using LLM-side query representations and MoE routing information and aligning them with retrieval-side candidate embedding is an effective and parameter-efficient strategy for mobile-applicable RAG chunk selection.

</details>


### [301] [On the Implicit Flatness Bias of Sharpness-Aware Minimization: A Linear Stability Analysis with Quantitative Hyperparameter Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03197)
*Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过线性稳定性分析证明了SAM算法在线性插值最小值附近的平坦度隐式偏差满足定量上界λ_max≤∛(bΓ/(2ρη²))，揭示了批量大小、学习率和扰动半径对平坦度的定量影响，并通过900个模型实验验证后提出TLC-SAM变体进一步降低Hessian最大特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM通过对抗扰动提高泛化性，但其对平坦最小值的隐式偏差的定量机制尚不清晰，且扰动半径ρ通常被视为孤立的调参变量，缺乏理论指导。

Method: 在局部线性化和梯度噪声对齐假设下，对mini-batch SAM在插值最小值附近进行线性稳定性分析，推导出Hessian最大特征值的定量上界；并在CIFAR-100上使用ResNet-18和VGG-19进行900个模型的控制实验验证；最后提出TLC-SAM通过泰勒近似误差动态调整ρ。

Result: 推导出λ_max的上界公式，证明更小批量、更大学习率或更大ρ都能促进更平坦的最小值；实验验证了增大ρ与更小Hessian最大特征值的关联；TLC-SAM相比固定半径SAM进一步降低了top Hessian特征值。

Conclusion: 提供了SAM的超参数定量界以及稳定性-局部性的分析视角，为设计和分析SAM变体提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) improves generalization by seeking parameters whose loss is robust to local adversarial perturbations, but the quantitative mechanism underlying its implicit bias toward flat minima remains unclear. In particular, the perturbation radius $ρ$ is typically treated as an isolated tuning parameter, despite defining the neighborhood in which SAM measures sharpness. We analyze mini-batch SAM near an interpolating minimum through linear stability. Under local linearization and gradient-noise alignment assumptions, we prove that every linearly stable minimum satisfies $λ_{\max}\leq\sqrt[3]{bΓ/(2ρη^2)}$, where $λ_{\max}$ is the largest Hessian eigenvalue, $b$ is the batch size, $η$ is the learning rate, and $Γ$ bounds the gradient norm. The bound quantitatively characterizes SAM's implicit flatness bias: holding the other quantities fixed, a smaller batch size, a larger learning rate, or a larger radius restricts linearly stable SAM to flatter minima. It also exposes a necessary trade-off: $ρ$ should be large enough to promote flatness, yet remain local enough to preserve the approximation and stable training. We validate this prediction in a controlled study of 900 models on CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18 and VGG-19, where increasing $ρ$ is consistently associated with a smaller largest Hessian eigenvalue across batch-size and learning-rate settings. Finally, we instantiate the analysis in Taylor-Locality Controlled SAM (TLC-SAM), which adjusts $ρ$ using the observed Taylor-approximation error and further reduces the top Hessian eigenvalue relative to fixed-radius SAM. Our results provide quantitative hyperparameter bounds and a stability--locality perspective for analyzing and designing SAM variants.

</details>


### [302] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03223)
*Ranxu Zhang,Guinan Chen,Chenshaodong,Jinghao Lin,Xiaozhou Xu,Sunzhe,Yanyong Zhang,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADRS框架，通过自蒸馏奖励塑形为多轮语言智能体构建与回报关联的token级信用分配，在多个交互式基准上显著提升长时程任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏轨迹级奖励虽然能指示成功与否，但无法识别哪些中间决策值得奖励，导致信用分配困难；现有方法未能联合校准教师分数、关联教师置信度与回报，也未能将信号整合到原生优势构建中。

Method: ADRS框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 对每步内部的特权token分数进行中心化和归一化；(2) 基于组内置信度-回报关联的Teacher Value Advantage (TVA)门控机制对token信号进行调制；(3) 将门控后的token信号整合到原生强化学习信用构建中。训练和推理过程中保持无技能依赖的rollout。

Result: 在三个交互式基准上的实验表明，ADRS在长时程任务上持续提升性能，且增益在多种RL骨干、减少数据场景、未见任务和延长训练下保持稳定。

Conclusion: ADRS通过自蒸馏奖励塑形有效解决了稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题，同时保持推理时无额外开销，为多轮语言智能体的强化学习提供了可扩展的密集监督方案。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning enables LLM agents to learn through interaction, but sparse trajectory-level rewards reveal success without identifying which intermediate decisions deserve credit. Training-only privileged skills can provide denser supervision by allowing the same frozen policy snapshot to rescore fixed tokens from skill-free trajectories while conditioned on task-matched procedural skills. Existing methods, however, do not jointly calibrate teacher scores across interaction steps, relate teacher confidence to realized returns, and integrate the resulting signal into native reward-to-advantage construction. We introduce Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Self-Distilled Reward Shaping (ADRS), a framework for constructing return-associated token-level credit for multi-turn language agents. ADRS centers and normalizes privileged token scores within each step, modulates them with a return-associated Teacher Value Advantage (TVA) gate based on within-group confidence--return association, and incorporates the gated token signal into native RL credit construction. Together, these components determine what the teacher prefers, when that preference is return-relevant, and how it enters the native reinforcement-learning credit path, while keeping rollouts and inference skill-free. Finally, experiments across three interactive benchmarks show that ADRS consistently improves performance on long-horizon tasks, with gains persisting across RL backbones, reduced-data settings, unseen tasks, and extended training. For anonymous review, our code is available at the following the link: https://github.com/gitrxh/ADRS-arxiv

</details>


### [303] [SAKI: Score-Aware Low-Rank Key Indexing for Long-Context KV Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03228)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAKI，一种无需训练的KV缓存索引方法，直接优化注意力分数而非键重建质量。通过推导秩r键压缩导致的期望注意力分数失真，得到协方差加权的低秩目标，其最优解具有闭合形式的非对称分解。实验表明SAKI在多个模型上优于键PCA，显著降低top-k召回误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩KV缓存方法要么保留模型权重，要么保留键方差，但这些都不能直接反映推理过程中实际使用的注意力分数。作者希望设计一种直接以注意力分数保真度为目标的方法，从而提高缓存压缩后的检索质量。

Method: 推导秩r键压缩下期望注意力分数失真的解析形式，证明其对应协方差加权的低秩目标；在边界条件下，控制该失真同时能改善top-k召回。最优秩r解由协方差加权查询-键算子的SVD得到闭合形式的非对称因子分解，由此构建SAKI索引。

Result: 在LLaMA 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、Mistral 7B v0.1和Llama 3.2 3B上，SAKI在所有测试秩下均优于键PCA。在秩32时，消除了PCA剩余top-64召回误差的13%-30%；LLaMA 3.1 8B从0.748提升到0.799，Qwen 2.5 7B从0.786提升到0.850。预测的分数MSE减少与实测高度一致（Pearson相关系数0.997）。消融实验确认增益来自注意力分数目标优化而非协方差加权本身。

Conclusion: SAKI通过直接优化注意力分数而非键重建质量，在KV缓存低秩压缩中取得显著改进。对打分算子的分析还解释了为何仅加权权重、不变子空间和键重建方法可能次优。

Abstract: Existing low rank KV cache methods preserve either model weights or key variance, neither of which directly reflects the attention scores used during inference. We derive the expected attention score distortion caused by rank r key compression and show that it yields a covariance weighted low rank objective. Under a margin condition, controlling this distortion also improves top k recall. The optimal rank r solution has a closed form asymmetric factorization obtained from the SVD of the covariance weighted query key operator. This motivates SAKI, a training free KV cache index that directly preserves attention scores rather than key reconstruction quality.
  Across LLaMA 3.1 8B, Qwen 2.5 7B, Mistral 7B v0.1, and Llama 3.2 3B, SAKI outperforms key PCA at every tested rank. At rank 32, it removes 13 to 30 percent of PCA's remaining top 64 recall error, including improvements from 0.748 to 0.799 on LLaMA 3.1 8B and from 0.786 to 0.850 on Qwen 2.5 7B. It improves 68 to 89 percent of attention heads per model, with the largest gains in deeper layers. Predicted score MSE reductions closely match empirical measurements, with a Pearson correlation of 0.997, while ablation studies confirm that the gains arise from optimizing the attention score objective rather than covariance weighting alone. Analysis of the scoring operator further explains why weight only, invariant subspace, and key reconstruction methods can be suboptimal.

</details>


### [304] [FinVerse: Financial Time-Series Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03259)
*Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Seunghan Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FinVerse，一个金融领域的时间序列预测基准，包含116,897个金融时间序列（1.711亿观测值），定义了11个指标族共78个评估指标，并根据每个序列的经济含义分配最合适的评估指标。对43个公开时间序列基础模型的评估表明，通用预测基准上的强性能并不一定能转化为有用的金融预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测基准通常用统一的误差指标评估异构序列，在通用指标上表现优异的模型未必能在真实世界决策（如股票涨跌预测）中产生好的结果，因此需要领域感知的评估基准。

Method: 构建金融领域时间序列基准FinVerse，包含116,897个金融序列（1.711亿观测值），从中选取60,232个经济相关序列作为评估目标；定义11个指标族共78个评估指标，根据序列的经济含义分配最合适的评估指标；对43个公开时间序列基础模型进行评估。

Result: 对43个时间序列基础模型的分析表明，在通用预测标准下表现强劲的模型并不一定能转化为有用的金融预测，揭示了通用评估与领域实际需求之间的差距。

Conclusion: 需要领域感知的基准来评估更接近实际决策目标的模型性能，FinVerse是第一歩朝着更现实的时间序列评估迈进的工作。

Abstract: As time-series foundation models have emerged, the need for benchmarks that can evaluate their forecasting ability in meaningful ways has become increasingly important. Existing time-series forecasting benchmarks provide useful standardized comparisons, but they often evaluate heterogeneous series with uniform error-based metrics. Strong performance under such metrics does not necessarily imply that a model's forecasts will support the best real-world decisions across domains. For example, in stock forecasting, correctly predicting whether a price will rise or fall can be more directly relevant to realized returns than minimizing point-wise forecast error alone. To this end, we introduce FinVerse, a finance-domain time-series forecasting benchmark that takes a first step toward more realistic evaluation. The released FinVerse data artifact contains 116,897 financial time series with 171.1M observations, of which 60,232 series with 17.4M observations are selected as evaluated targets based on their economic relevance to financial decisions. Unlike generic forecasting benchmarks that primarily emphasize uniform point-forecast or probabilistic accuracy, FinVerse defines 11 metric families comprising 78 evaluation metrics and assigns the most appropriate evaluation metrics to each individual time series based on its underlying economic meaning. Our analysis of 43 public time-series forecasting foundation models shows that strong performance under generic forecasting criteria does not necessarily translate into useful financial forecasts. This finding highlights the need for domain-aware benchmarks that evaluate models under objectives closer to real-world decision making.

</details>


### [305] [ED-DiT: Physics-Guided Diffusion Pretraining for Transferable Molecular Representations from Electron Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03260)
*Liang Shuang,Haocheng Wang,Jiayi Song,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei*

Main category: cs.LG
